```go
	ConsoleView Permission = "console.view"
```

## Task 9: Roles held by staff in a scope, and the owner role kept by the service and the database

D3, D6 and D7: `staff.Roles` makes, edits, deletes, assigns and removes roles, each change
audited with the staff member as the actor, and is the console's `Authoriser` in place of `Owners`. The
owner holds every declared permission by `role.owner` (no rows), anybody else what a role grants where it
is assigned; the console's own permissions count wherever a role is assigned, since its pages belong to
every scope. The owner role cannot be edited, deleted, assigned or stripped: the service refuses, and so
does the database to the application role writing SQL of its own — constraints first (a composite key
that makes an assignment say truthfully whether it is the owner's, one owner assignment for the whole
platform), triggers for what constraints cannot say. Only staff hold roles, which the database now
enforces too.

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00019_roles.sql`, `internal/staff/roles.go`, `internal/staff/roles_integration_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/staff/bootstrap.go`, `internal/staff/bootstrap_integration_test.go`, `internal/staff/staff.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/console.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/console_integration_test.go`, `cmd/marketplace/main.go`, `docs/infrastructure.md`

  `ErrRoleNameMissing`, `ErrRoleNameInvalid`, `ErrRoleNameTaken`, `ErrPermissionUnknown`, `ErrNotStaff`,

Create `internal/staff/roles_integration_test.go`:

```go
//go:build integration

package staff_test

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/tenancy"
)

// team is a fresh database with an owner, a colleague who holds no role, two
// marketplaces and the roles' service.
type team struct {
	database  serving
	service   *identity.Service
	trail     *recording
	roles     *staff.Roles
	owner     string
	colleague string
	one, two  string
}

func newTeam(t *testing.T) team {
	t.Helper()
	database, service, trail := fresh(t)
	if _, err := staff.NewBootstrap(database, service, trail, token).
		Setup(t.Context(), platform(), token, "Owner", "owner@example.test", password); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tm := team{database: database, service: service, trail: trail, roles: staff.NewRoles(database, trail)}
	tm.owner = tm.staffID(t, "owner@example.test")
	tm.colleague = tm.newStaff(t, "Colleague", "colleague@example.test")
	if err := database.pool.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var specs []tenancy.Spec
		for _, slug := range []string{"one", "two"} {
			specs = append(specs, tenancy.Spec{Slug: slug, Name: strings.ToUpper(slug[:1]) + slug[1:], Market: "BR",
				RevenueModel: "commission", State: "active", DefaultLanguage: "pt-BR", Languages: []string{"pt-BR"},
				Hosts: []string{slug + ".test"}})
		}
		applied, err := tenancy.Seed(t.Context(), tx, specs)
		if err == nil {
			tm.one, tm.two = applied[0].ID, applied[1].ID
		}
		return err
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return tm
}

// newStaff creates a staff account and returns its id.
func (tm team) newStaff(t *testing.T, name, email string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var id string
	if _, err := tm.service.CreateStaff(t.Context(), platform(), name, email, password,
		func(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, account string) error { id = account; return nil }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return id
}

func (tm team) staffID(t *testing.T, email string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var id string
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT id::text FROM account WHERE email_normalised = $1`, email).Scan(&id)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return id
}

// by is an actor: the staff member signed in, from where.
func by(account string) staff.Actor {
	return staff.Actor{Account: account, IP: "203.0.113.5", UserAgent: "test"}
}

// ownerRole is the owner role's id.
func (tm team) ownerRole(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	roles, err := tm.roles.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if len(roles) == 0 || !roles[0].Owner {
		t.Fatalf("the owner role is not listed first: %+v", roles)
	}
	return roles[0].ID
}

// allows fails the test unless account is allowed permission in scope
// exactly when want says so.
func (tm team) allows(t *testing.T, account string, permission staff.Permission, scope string, want bool) {
	t.Helper()
	got, err := tm.roles.Allows(t.Context(), account, permission, scope)
	if err != nil || got != want {
		t.Errorf("Allows(%s, %q) = %v, %v; want %v", permission, scope, got, err, want)
	}
}

// state is an audit entry's After, or its Before when it has none.
func state(t *testing.T, e audit.Entry) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	raw := e.After
	if len(raw) == 0 {
		raw = e.Before
	}
	var decoded map[string]any
	if err := json.Unmarshal(raw, &decoded); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("%s carries %s: %v", e.Action, raw, err)
	}
	return decoded
}

// A role is a name and declared permissions. The names are the owner's
// words, compared without case or surrounding spaces; the permissions are
// the registry's, kept in its order. Every creation is audited with the
// staff member as the actor.
func TestARoleIsANameAndDeclaredPermissions(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "  Support ", []staff.Permission{staff.StaffInvite, staff.ConsoleView, staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Create = %v", err)
	}
	role, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), id)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if role.Name != "Support" || role.Owner || !slices.Equal(role.Permissions, []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffInvite}) {
		t.Fatalf("the role is %+v", role)
	}
	roles, err := tm.roles.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil || len(roles) != 2 || !roles[0].Owner || roles[1].ID != id {
		t.Fatalf("List = %+v, %v; want the owner role, then Support", roles, err)
	}

	for _, refused := range []struct {
		name        string
		permissions []staff.Permission
		want        error
	}{
		{"support", nil, staff.ErrRoleNameTaken},
		{"OWNER", nil, staff.ErrRoleNameTaken},
		{"   ", nil, staff.ErrRoleNameMissing},
		{strings.Repeat("r", staff.MaxRoleNameLength+1), nil, staff.ErrRoleNameInvalid},
		{"Line\nbreak", nil, staff.ErrRoleNameInvalid},
		{"Everything", []staff.Permission{"roles.everything"}, staff.ErrPermissionUnknown},
	} {
		if _, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), refused.name, refused.permissions); !errors.Is(err, refused.want) {
			t.Errorf("Create(%q, %v) = %v, want %v", refused.name, refused.permissions, err, refused.want)
		}
	}

	created, found := tm.trail.find("staff.role_created")
	if !found || created.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: tm.owner, Kind: audit.Staff}) || created.Subject.ID != id ||
		created.From != "203.0.113.5" || state(t, created)["name"] != "Support" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_created = %+v (found %v)", created, found)
	}
}

// Editing a role changes its name and its permissions, and the audit keeps
// both what it was and what it became.
func TestARoleIsEdited(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Support", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, "Help desk", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleVersion, staff.ConsoleView}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Edit = %v", err)
	}
	role, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), id)
	if err != nil || role.Name != "Help desk" || !slices.Equal(role.Permissions, []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.ConsoleVersion}) {
		t.Fatalf("the edited role is %+v, %v", role, err)
	}
	edited, found := tm.trail.find("staff.role_edited")
	if !found || edited.Actor.ID != tm.owner {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_edited = %+v (found %v)", edited, found)
	}
	var before, after map[string]any
	if json.Unmarshal(edited.Before, &before) != nil || json.Unmarshal(edited.After, &after) != nil ||
		before["name"] != "Support" || after["name"] != "Help desk" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_edited carries %s, then %s", edited.Before, edited.After)
	}
	for _, missing := range []string{"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009", "not-an-id"} {
		if err := tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), missing, "Nobody", nil); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound) {
			t.Errorf("Edit(%q) = %v, want ErrRoleNotFound", missing, err)
		}
		if _, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), missing); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound) {
			t.Errorf("Role(%q) = %v, want ErrRoleNotFound", missing, err)
		}
	}
}

// A role is assigned with a scope and removed again, each audited about the
// staff member it was given to; a role somebody holds is not deleted, and
// one nobody holds is.
func TestOnlyARoleNobodyHoldsIsDeleted(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Support", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Assign = %v", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, tm.one); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrAlreadyAssigned) {
		t.Fatalf("the same assignment twice = %v, want ErrAlreadyAssigned", err)
	}
	role, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), id)
	if err != nil || len(role.Holders) != 1 || role.Holders[0].Account != tm.colleague ||
		role.Holders[0].Marketplace != tm.one || role.Holders[0].MarketplaceName != "One" || role.Holders[0].Name != "Colleague" {
		t.Fatalf("the role's holders are %+v, %v", role.Holders, err)
	}
	assigned, found := tm.trail.find("staff.role_assigned")
	if !found || assigned.Actor.ID != tm.owner || assigned.Subject != (audit.Subject{Kind: "account", ID: tm.colleague, Person: tm.colleague}) ||
		state(t, assigned)["scope"] != tm.one || state(t, assigned)["role"] != id {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_assigned = %+v (found %v)", assigned, found)
	}

	if err := tm.roles.Delete(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleHeld) {
		t.Fatalf("deleting a held role = %v, want ErrRoleHeld", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Unassign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, tm.two); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAssigned) {
		t.Fatalf("removing an assignment in another scope = %v, want ErrNotAssigned", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Unassign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Unassign = %v", err)
	}
	if removed, found := tm.trail.find("staff.role_unassigned"); !found || removed.Subject.ID != tm.colleague || state(t, removed)["scope"] != tm.one {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_unassigned = %+v (found %v)", removed, found)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Delete(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("deleting a role nobody holds = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), id); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("the deleted role = %v, want ErrRoleNotFound", err)
	}
	if deleted, found := tm.trail.find("staff.role_deleted"); !found || deleted.Subject.ID != id || state(t, deleted)["name"] != "Support" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_deleted = %+v (found %v)", deleted, found)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, ""); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound) {
		t.Fatalf("assigning a deleted role = %v, want ErrRoleNotFound", err)
	}
}

// A role assigned for one marketplace allows its permissions there and
// nowhere else; assigned for the whole platform, everywhere. The console's
// own permissions, its home and its build, are held on the console's own
// pages wherever the role is assigned (D3, D6).
func TestAnAssignmentAllowsItsPermissionsInItsScope(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	for _, permission := range staff.Permissions() {
		tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, permission, staff.Platform, false)
	}
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Support", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffInvite})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.StaffInvite, tm.one, true)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.StaffInvite, tm.two, false)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.StaffInvite, staff.Platform, false)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.ConsoleView, staff.Platform, true)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.ConsoleView, tm.two, false)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.ConsoleVersion, staff.Platform, false)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.RolesManage, tm.one, false)

	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, staff.Platform); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.StaffInvite, tm.two, true)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.StaffInvite, staff.Platform, true)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.RolesManage, staff.Platform, false)

	// The owner holds every declared permission everywhere by the role,
	// with no row saying so, and nothing the code does not declare.
	for _, permission := range staff.Permissions() {
		for _, scope := range []string{staff.Platform, tm.one} {
			tm.allows(t, tm.owner, permission, scope, true)
		}
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.owner, "roles.everything", staff.Platform, false)
	var rows int
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT count(*) FROM role_permission p JOIN role r ON r.id = p.role_id WHERE r.owner`).Scan(&rows)
	}); err != nil || rows != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the owner role has %d permission rows (%v), want none", rows, err)
	}
}

// Only a staff member who may manage roles across the platform changes
// them: nobody without the permission, and nobody who holds it for one
// marketplace only, since a role is the platform's.
func TestOnlyWhoManagesRolesChangesThem(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	if _, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.colleague), "Mine", nil); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAllowed) {
		t.Fatalf("Create by a colleague with no role = %v, want ErrNotAllowed", err)
	}
	managers, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Managers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.RolesManage})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), managers, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.colleague), "Mine", nil); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAllowed) {
		t.Fatalf("Create by a colleague who manages roles in one marketplace = %v, want ErrNotAllowed", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), managers, tm.colleague, staff.Platform); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.colleague), "Mine", nil); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Create by a colleague who manages roles = %v", err)
	}
}

// A role is held by staff only: the service does not see anybody else, and
// the database refuses the row even when the service is bypassed.
func TestOnlyStaffHoldRoles(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Support", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var buyer string
	if err := tm.database.pool.QueryRow(t.Context(), `
		INSERT INTO account (marketplace_id, kind, email, email_normalised, name)
		VALUES ($1, 'buyer', 'buyer@example.test', 'buyer@example.test', 'Buyer') RETURNING id::text`, tm.one).Scan(&buyer); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, account := range []string{buyer, "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009", "not-an-id"} {
		if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, account, staff.Platform); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotStaff) {
			t.Errorf("assigning to %q = %v, want ErrNotStaff", account, err)
		}
	}
	for statement, code := range map[string]string{
		`INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id) VALUES ($1, $2)`:                        "23503",
		`INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id, account_kind) VALUES ($1, $2, 'buyer')`: "23514",
	} {
		wantRefused(t, tm.database, code, statement, buyer, id)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), id, tm.colleague, "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009"); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrScopeUnknown) {
		t.Fatalf("assigning for a marketplace that does not exist = %v, want ErrScopeUnknown", err)
	}
}

// wantRefused runs statement as the application role, on the platform, and
// fails the test unless the database refuses it with code.
func wantRefused(t *testing.T, database serving, code, statement string, args ...any) {
	t.Helper()
	err := database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.Exec(t.Context(), statement, args...)
		return err
	})
	var refused *pgconn.PgError
	if !errors.As(err, &refused) || refused.Code != code {
		t.Errorf("%s = %v, want SQLSTATE %s", statement, err, code)
	}
}

// The service refuses to edit, delete, assign or remove the owner role, and
// the owner keeps every permission (D7).
func TestTheServiceKeepsTheOwnerRole(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	owner := tm.ownerRole(t)
	for what, err := range map[string]error{
		"edit":                  tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), owner, "Mine", nil),
		"delete":                tm.roles.Delete(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), owner),
		"assign to a colleague": tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), owner, tm.colleague, staff.Platform),
		"assign in a market":    tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), owner, tm.owner, tm.one),
		"remove from the owner": tm.roles.Unassign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), owner, tm.owner, staff.Platform),
	} {
		if !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnerRole) {
			t.Errorf("%s the owner role = %v, want ErrOwnerRole", what, err)
		}
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.owner, staff.RolesManage, staff.Platform, true)
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.RolesManage, staff.Platform, false)
}

// The database refuses the same, to the application role writing SQL of its
// own, so a mistake in the code cannot strip the owner: the owner role
// cannot be deleted or changed, no other role can become it, it is given no
// permission rows, its one assignment can be neither removed nor moved, and
// nobody else can be given it (D7).
func TestTheDatabaseKeepsTheOwnerRole(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	other, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Support", nil)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, refused := range []struct {
		code, statement string
		args            []any
	}{
		{"23001", `DELETE FROM role WHERE owner`, nil},
		{"23001", `UPDATE role SET name = 'mine' WHERE owner`, nil},
		{"23001", `UPDATE role SET owner = false WHERE owner`, nil},
		{"23001", `UPDATE role SET owner = true WHERE id = $1`, []any{other}},
		{"23505", `INSERT INTO role (name, owner) VALUES ('second owner', true)`, nil},
		{"23001", `INSERT INTO role_permission (role_id, permission_key) SELECT id, 'console.view' FROM role WHERE owner`, nil},
		{"23001", `DELETE FROM user_role WHERE owner`, nil},
		{"23001", `UPDATE user_role SET marketplace_id = $1 WHERE owner`, []any{tm.one}},
		{"23001", `UPDATE user_role SET account_id = $1 WHERE owner`, []any{tm.colleague}},
		{"23505", `INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id, owner) SELECT $1, id, true FROM role WHERE owner`, []any{tm.colleague}},
		{"23503", `INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id) SELECT $1, id FROM role WHERE owner`, []any{tm.colleague}},
		{"23001", `DELETE FROM user_role WHERE account_id = $1`, []any{tm.owner}},
	} {
		wantRefused(t, tm.database, refused.code, refused.statement, refused.args...)
	}
	// The owner's account cannot go either, while it holds the role.
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		_, err := tx.Exec(t.Context(), `DELETE FROM account WHERE id = $1`, tm.owner)
		return err
	}); err == nil {
		t.Error("the owner's account was deleted")
	}

	roles, err := tm.roles.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil || !roles[0].Owner || roles[0].Name != "owner" || len(roles[0].Holders) != 1 ||
		roles[0].Holders[0].Account != tm.owner || roles[0].Holders[0].Marketplace != staff.Platform {
		t.Fatalf("after the attempts the owner role is %+v, %v", roles, err)
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.owner, staff.RolesManage, staff.Platform, true)
}
```

Create `migrations/00019_roles.sql`:

```sql
-- Roles made in the console, held by staff only, and the owner role kept
-- whatever the code does (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md,
-- D6, D7).
--
-- The service refuses to edit, delete, assign or strip the owner role; this
-- migration makes the database refuse it again, to the application role
-- writing SQL of its own, so a mistake in the code cannot strip the owner:
--
-- * one owner role (role_one_owner, 00017) and one assignment of it
--   (user_role_one_owner), for the whole platform
--   (user_role_owner_is_platform_wide);
-- * `user_role.owner` says whether an assignment is of the owner role, and
--   the foreign key on (role_id, owner) makes it say so truthfully: the owner
--   role cannot be assigned as anything but the owner, so a second
--   assignment of it meets the unique index;
-- * the triggers refuse to delete or change the owner role, to make another
--   role the owner role, to give it permission rows (it holds every
--   permission by being the owner role, D7) and to delete or change its
--   assignment — which is also what keeps the owner's account from being
--   deleted, since the assignment would go with it.
--
-- A role is held by a staff account only: `user_role.account_kind` can only
-- be 'staff', and the foreign key on (account_id, account_kind) finds the
-- account only when it is one (F15 PR 1 left this to the code).

-- +goose Up

-- A role's name is the owner's words, compared without case: two roles
-- the console shows alike would be two roles nobody can tell apart.
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX role_name_is_unique ON role (lower(name));

ALTER TABLE account ADD CONSTRAINT account_id_and_kind_are_unique UNIQUE (id, kind);
ALTER TABLE user_role
    ADD COLUMN account_kind text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'staff'
        CONSTRAINT user_role_is_held_by_staff CHECK (account_kind = 'staff'),
    DROP CONSTRAINT user_role_account_id_fkey,
    ADD CONSTRAINT user_role_account_is_staff FOREIGN KEY (account_id, account_kind)
        REFERENCES account (id, kind) ON DELETE CASCADE;

ALTER TABLE role ADD CONSTRAINT role_id_and_owner_are_unique UNIQUE (id, owner);
ALTER TABLE user_role ADD COLUMN owner boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;
-- The first run's assignment, where there was one, is the owner's.
UPDATE user_role u SET owner = r.owner FROM role r WHERE r.id = u.role_id;
ALTER TABLE user_role
    DROP CONSTRAINT user_role_role_id_fkey,
    ADD CONSTRAINT user_role_role_is_as_marked FOREIGN KEY (role_id, owner)
        REFERENCES role (id, owner) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    ADD CONSTRAINT user_role_owner_is_platform_wide CHECK (NOT owner OR marketplace_id IS NULL);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX user_role_one_owner ON user_role (owner) WHERE owner;

-- restrict_violation, as a foreign key's ON DELETE RESTRICT would answer:
-- the change would take away something the owner role stands on.
-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION role_keeps_the_owner() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF OLD.owner THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'the owner role cannot be deleted or changed'
            USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'UPDATE' AND NEW.owner THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'no role becomes the owner role'
            USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        RETURN OLD;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION role_permission_keeps_the_owner() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    -- OLD is NULL on an insert and NEW on a delete, which IN ignores.
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM role WHERE owner AND id IN (OLD.role_id, NEW.role_id)) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'the owner role holds every permission by being the owner role'
            USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        RETURN OLD;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION user_role_keeps_the_owner() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF OLD.owner THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'the owner''s assignment cannot be removed or changed'
            USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    IF TG_OP = 'DELETE' THEN
        RETURN OLD;
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

CREATE TRIGGER role_keeps_the_owner
    BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON role
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION role_keeps_the_owner();
CREATE TRIGGER role_permission_keeps_the_owner
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OR DELETE ON role_permission
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION role_permission_keeps_the_owner();
CREATE TRIGGER user_role_keeps_the_owner
    BEFORE UPDATE OR DELETE ON user_role
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION user_role_keeps_the_owner();

-- +goose Down
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS user_role_keeps_the_owner ON user_role;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS role_permission_keeps_the_owner ON role_permission;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS role_keeps_the_owner ON role;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS user_role_keeps_the_owner();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS role_permission_keeps_the_owner();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS role_keeps_the_owner();

DROP INDEX IF EXISTS user_role_one_owner;
ALTER TABLE user_role
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS user_role_owner_is_platform_wide,
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS user_role_role_is_as_marked,
    ADD CONSTRAINT user_role_role_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (role_id) REFERENCES role (id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
    DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS owner;
ALTER TABLE role DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS role_id_and_owner_are_unique;

ALTER TABLE user_role
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS user_role_account_is_staff,
    ADD CONSTRAINT user_role_account_id_fkey FOREIGN KEY (account_id) REFERENCES account (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS account_kind;
ALTER TABLE account DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS account_id_and_kind_are_unique;

DROP INDEX IF EXISTS role_name_is_unique;
```

Create `internal/staff/roles.go`:

```go
package staff

import (
	"context"
	"encoding/json"
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"strings"
	"time"
	"unicode"
	"unicode/utf8"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgconn"
	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5/pgtype"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
)

// MaxRoleNameLength bounds a role's name, in code points after trimming:
// it is a label on a page, not a description.
const MaxRoleNameLength = 60

var (
	// ErrNotAllowed is a change by a staff member who may not manage roles
	// across the platform.
	ErrNotAllowed = errors.New("staff: not allowed to manage roles")
	// ErrRoleNotFound is a role that does not exist.
	ErrRoleNotFound = errors.New("staff: no such role")
	// ErrOwnerRole is a change to the owner role, which nobody makes (D7).
	ErrOwnerRole = errors.New("staff: the owner role cannot be edited, deleted, assigned or removed")
	// ErrRoleHeld is the deletion of a role somebody holds.
	ErrRoleHeld = errors.New("staff: the role is held")
	// ErrRoleNameMissing, ErrRoleNameInvalid and ErrRoleNameTaken are a name
	// left empty, one too long or not plain text, and one another role
	// already has.
	ErrRoleNameMissing = errors.New("staff: role name missing")
	ErrRoleNameInvalid = errors.New("staff: role name too long or not plain text")
	ErrRoleNameTaken   = errors.New("staff: role name taken")
	// ErrPermissionUnknown is a permission the code does not declare.
	ErrPermissionUnknown = errors.New("staff: unknown permission")
	// ErrNotStaff is an assignment to an account that is not a staff
	// member's.
	ErrNotStaff = errors.New("staff: only staff hold roles")
	// ErrScopeUnknown is an assignment for a marketplace that does not
	// exist.
	ErrScopeUnknown = errors.New("staff: no such marketplace")
	// ErrAlreadyAssigned and ErrNotAssigned are an assignment that exists
	// already, and the removal of one that does not.
	ErrAlreadyAssigned = errors.New("staff: the role is already assigned")
	ErrNotAssigned     = errors.New("staff: the role is not assigned")
)

// Role is a role as the console shows it: its name and its permissions, and
// who holds it where. The owner role lists no permission: it holds every
// one, including those declared after it was made (D7).
type Role struct {
	ID          string
	Name        string
	Owner       bool
	Permissions []Permission
	Holders     []Assignment
}

// Assignment is a role held by a staff member, in a scope: one marketplace,
// or the whole platform (Marketplace is Platform).
type Assignment struct {
	Account         string
	Name            string
	Email           string
	Marketplace     string
	MarketplaceName string
	AssignedAt      time.Time
}

// Member is a staff member a role can be assigned to.
type Member struct {
	ID    string
	Name  string
	Email string
}

// Scope is a marketplace a role can be assigned in.
type Scope struct {
	ID   string
	Name string
}

// Actor is the staff member making a change, and where from: what the
// audit records as its actor.
type Actor struct {
	Account   string
	IP        string
	UserAgent string
}

// Roles are the console's roles and who holds them, and the Authoriser
// that answers from them (D3, D6, D7). Every change is made by a staff member
// who may manage roles across the platform, in one transaction with its
// audit record, the staff member as its actor.
type Roles struct {
	db    Transactor
	audit Auditor
	now   func() time.Time
}

// NewRoles returns the roles, read and written through db, audited to
// auditor.
func NewRoles(db Transactor, auditor Auditor) *Roles {
	return &Roles{db: db, audit: auditor, now: func() time.Time { return time.Now().UTC() }}
}

// shell is the permissions to the console itself rather than to what it
// manages: its home and its build. The console's own pages belong to every
// scope, so a role that grants one of these anywhere grants it there too —
// a staff member of one marketplace signs in to the same console as one of
// the whole platform.
var shell = []Permission{ConsoleView, ConsoleVersion}

// Allows reports whether account may do permission in marketplace, or
// across the platform when marketplace is Platform: the owner may do every
// declared permission everywhere, and anybody else what a role of theirs
// grants in that marketplace or across the platform (D3). A permission the
// code does not declare is allowed to nobody.
func (r *Roles) Allows(ctx context.Context, account string, permission Permission, marketplace string) (bool, error) {
	if !Known(permission) || !validID(account) || (marketplace != Platform && !validID(marketplace)) {
		return false, nil
	}
	var allowed bool
	err := r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var err error
		allowed, err = allows(ctx, tx, account, permission, marketplace)
		return err
	})
	return allowed, err
}

// allows is Allows inside a transaction on the platform.
func allows(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account string, permission Permission, marketplace string) (bool, error) {
	anywhere := marketplace == Platform && slices.Contains(shell, permission)
	var allowed bool
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT EXISTS (
		    SELECT 1 FROM user_role u JOIN role r ON r.id = u.role_id
		     WHERE u.account_id = $1
		       AND (u.marketplace_id IS NULL OR u.marketplace_id = nullif($3, '')::uuid OR $4)
		       AND (r.owner OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM role_permission p
		                                WHERE p.role_id = r.id AND p.permission_key = $2)))`,
		account, string(permission), marketplace, anywhere).Scan(&allowed)
	return allowed, err
}

// validID reports whether id is written as a UUID, which every identifier
// here is: anything else names nothing, and is answered so before the
// database is asked to cast it.
func validID(id string) bool {
	var parsed pgtype.UUID
	return parsed.Scan(id) == nil
}

// CheckRoleName returns a role's name, trimmed, or why it cannot be one.
func CheckRoleName(name string) (string, error) {
	name = strings.TrimSpace(name)
	if name == "" {
		return "", ErrRoleNameMissing
	}
	if !utf8.ValidString(name) || utf8.RuneCountInString(name) > MaxRoleNameLength {
		return "", ErrRoleNameInvalid
	}
	for _, c := range name {
		if unicode.IsControl(c) || unicode.Is(unicode.Cf, c) {
			return "", ErrRoleNameInvalid
		}
	}
	return name, nil
}

// checkPermissions returns the permissions given, each once and in the
// registry's order, or ErrPermissionUnknown.
func checkPermissions(given []Permission) ([]Permission, error) {
	for _, p := range given {
		if !Known(p) {
			return nil, ErrPermissionUnknown
		}
	}
	var ordered []Permission
	for _, p := range registry {
		if slices.Contains(given, p) {
			ordered = append(ordered, p)
		}
	}
	return ordered, nil
}

// change runs fn in a transaction on the platform, once actor is known to
// be allowed to manage roles there.
func (r *Roles) change(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, fn func(tx pgx.Tx) error) error {
	if !validID(actor.Account) {
		return ErrNotAllowed
	}
	return r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		allowed, err := allows(ctx, tx, actor.Account, RolesManage, Platform)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !allowed {
			return ErrNotAllowed
		}
		return fn(tx)
	})
}

// Create makes a role of name and permissions, and returns its id.
func (r *Roles) Create(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, name string, permissions []Permission) (string, error) {
	name, err := CheckRoleName(name)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	permissions, err = checkPermissions(permissions)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	var id string
	err = r.change(ctx, actor, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `INSERT INTO role (name, created_at) VALUES ($1, $2) RETURNING id::text`,
			name, r.now()).Scan(&id)
		if violates(err, "role_name_is_unique") {
			return ErrRoleNameTaken
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := grant(ctx, tx, id, permissions); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return r.record(ctx, tx, actor, "staff.role_created", audit.Subject{Kind: "role", ID: id},
			nil, described{"name": name, "permissions": permissions})
	})
	return id, err
}

// Edit gives role another name and another set of permissions.
func (r *Roles) Edit(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, role, name string, permissions []Permission) error {
	name, err := CheckRoleName(name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	permissions, err = checkPermissions(permissions)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	return r.change(ctx, actor, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		before, err := lockRole(ctx, tx, role, "FOR UPDATE")
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE role SET name = $2 WHERE id = $1`, role, name)
		if violates(err, "role_name_is_unique") {
			return ErrRoleNameTaken
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM role_permission WHERE role_id = $1`, role); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := grant(ctx, tx, role, permissions); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return r.record(ctx, tx, actor, "staff.role_edited", audit.Subject{Kind: "role", ID: role},
			described{"name": before.Name, "permissions": before.Permissions},
			described{"name": name, "permissions": permissions})
	})
}

// Delete removes role, which nobody may hold.
func (r *Roles) Delete(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, role string) error {
	return r.change(ctx, actor, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		before, err := lockRole(ctx, tx, role, "FOR UPDATE")
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var held bool
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = $1)`, role).Scan(&held); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if held {
			return ErrRoleHeld
		}
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `DELETE FROM role WHERE id = $1`, role); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return r.record(ctx, tx, actor, "staff.role_deleted", audit.Subject{Kind: "role", ID: role},
			described{"name": before.Name, "permissions": before.Permissions}, nil)
	})
}

// Assign gives role to the staff member account, in marketplace or across
// the platform (Platform).
func (r *Roles) Assign(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, role, account, marketplace string) error {
	if !validID(account) {
		return ErrNotStaff
	}
	if marketplace != Platform && !validID(marketplace) {
		return ErrScopeUnknown
	}
	return r.change(ctx, actor, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := lockRole(ctx, tx, role, "FOR SHARE"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		// The platform's transaction sees staff accounts and nobody else's.
		var staff bool
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM account WHERE id = $1 AND kind = 'staff')`,
			account).Scan(&staff); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !staff {
			return ErrNotStaff
		}
		tag, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id, marketplace_id, assigned_by, assigned_at)
			VALUES ($1, $2, nullif($3, '')::uuid, $4, $5)
			ON CONFLICT ON CONSTRAINT user_role_is_unique DO NOTHING`,
			account, role, marketplace, actor.Account, r.now())
		if violates(err, "user_role_marketplace_id_fkey") {
			return ErrScopeUnknown
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
			return ErrAlreadyAssigned
		}
		return r.record(ctx, tx, actor, "staff.role_assigned", audit.Subject{Kind: "account", ID: account, Person: account},
			nil, described{"role": role, "scope": scopeName(marketplace)})
	})
}

// Unassign takes role back from the staff member account, in marketplace or
// across the platform.
func (r *Roles) Unassign(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, role, account, marketplace string) error {
	if !validID(account) || (marketplace != Platform && !validID(marketplace)) {
		return ErrNotAssigned
	}
	return r.change(ctx, actor, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if _, err := lockRole(ctx, tx, role, "FOR SHARE"); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		tag, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
			DELETE FROM user_role
			 WHERE account_id = $1 AND role_id = $2 AND marketplace_id IS NOT DISTINCT FROM nullif($3, '')::uuid`,
			account, role, marketplace)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if tag.RowsAffected() == 0 {
			return ErrNotAssigned
		}
		return r.record(ctx, tx, actor, "staff.role_unassigned", audit.Subject{Kind: "account", ID: account, Person: account},
			described{"role": role, "scope": scopeName(marketplace)}, nil)
	})
}

// scopeName is how the audit writes a scope.
func scopeName(marketplace string) string {
	if marketplace == Platform {
		return "platform"
	}
	return marketplace
}

// lockRole reads role and its permissions with lock ("FOR UPDATE" or "FOR
// SHARE"), refusing a role that does not exist and the owner role, which
// nothing here changes (D7).
func lockRole(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, role, lock string) (Role, error) {
	if !validID(role) {
		return Role{}, ErrRoleNotFound
	}
	var found Role
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT id::text, name, owner FROM role WHERE id = $1 `+lock, role).
		Scan(&found.ID, &found.Name, &found.Owner)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return Role{}, ErrRoleNotFound
	}
	if err != nil {
		return Role{}, err
	}
	if found.Owner {
		return Role{}, ErrOwnerRole
	}
	found.Permissions, err = collect(ctx, tx, `SELECT permission_key FROM role_permission WHERE role_id = $1`, role)
	if err != nil {
		return Role{}, err
	}
	found.Permissions, _ = checkPermissions(found.Permissions)
	return found, nil
}

// grant writes role's permissions.
func grant(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, role string, permissions []Permission) error {
	keys := make([]string, len(permissions))
	for i, p := range permissions {
		keys[i] = string(p)
	}
	_, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `INSERT INTO role_permission (role_id, permission_key) SELECT $1, unnest($2::text[])`, role, keys)
	return err
}

// violates reports whether err is the database refusing a row by the named
// constraint.
func violates(err error, constraint string) bool {
	var refused *pgconn.PgError
	return errors.As(err, &refused) && refused.ConstraintName == constraint
}

// described is what an audit record says a role or an assignment was, or
// became.
type described map[string]any

// record audits a change by actor, on the platform's chain.
func (r *Roles) record(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, actor Actor, action string, subject audit.Subject, before, after described) error {
	entry := audit.Entry{Actor: audit.Actor{ID: actor.Account, Kind: audit.Staff}, Action: action,
		Subject: subject, From: actor.IP}
	for _, side := range []struct {
		state described
		into  *json.RawMessage
	}{{before, &entry.Before}, {after, &entry.After}} {
		if side.state == nil {
			continue
		}
		side.state["user_agent"] = actor.UserAgent
		raw, err := json.Marshal(side.state)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		*side.into = raw
	}
	return r.audit.Append(ctx, tx, entry)
}

// List returns every role, the owner role first and the others by name,
// with their permissions and who holds them where.
func (r *Roles) List(ctx context.Context) ([]Role, error) {
	var roles []Role
	err := r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var err error
		roles, err = readRoles(ctx, tx, "")
		return err
	})
	return roles, err
}

// Role returns one role, with its permissions and who holds it where.
func (r *Roles) Role(ctx context.Context, id string) (Role, error) {
	if !validID(id) {
		return Role{}, ErrRoleNotFound
	}
	var roles []Role
	err := r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		var err error
		roles, err = readRoles(ctx, tx, id)
		return err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return Role{}, err
	}
	if len(roles) == 0 {
		return Role{}, ErrRoleNotFound
	}
	return roles[0], nil
}

// readRoles reads the role id, or every role when id is "".
func readRoles(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, id string) ([]Role, error) {
	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `
		SELECT r.id::text, r.name, r.owner,
		       coalesce(array_agg(p.permission_key) FILTER (WHERE p.permission_key IS NOT NULL), '{}')
		  FROM role r LEFT JOIN role_permission p ON p.role_id = r.id
		 WHERE $1 = '' OR r.id = nullif($1, '')::uuid
		 GROUP BY r.id
		 ORDER BY r.owner DESC, lower(r.name)`, id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	roles, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, func(row pgx.CollectableRow) (Role, error) {
		var role Role
		var keys []string
		err := row.Scan(&role.ID, &role.Name, &role.Owner, &keys)
		for _, key := range keys {
			role.Permissions = append(role.Permissions, Permission(key))
		}
		role.Permissions, _ = checkPermissions(role.Permissions)
		return role, err
	})
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	holders, err := readHolders(ctx, tx, id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	for i := range roles {
		roles[i].Holders = holders[roles[i].ID]
	}
	return roles, nil
}

// readHolders reads who holds role id, or every role when id is "", by
// role. A marketplace is named as the host map names it: the platform's
// transaction reads no marketplace's rows, and the host map is the one way
// to read them all (migrations/00004_row_level_security.sql).
func readHolders(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, id string) (map[string][]Assignment, error) {
	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `
		SELECT u.role_id::text, a.id::text, a.name, a.email,
		       coalesce(u.marketplace_id::text, ''), coalesce(m.name, ''), u.assigned_at
		  FROM user_role u
		  JOIN account a ON a.id = u.account_id
		  LEFT JOIN (SELECT DISTINCT marketplace_id, name FROM tenancy_host_map()) m
		         ON m.marketplace_id = u.marketplace_id::text
		 WHERE $1 = '' OR u.role_id = nullif($1, '')::uuid
		 ORDER BY lower(a.name), a.id, u.marketplace_id NULLS FIRST`, id)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, err
	}
	defer rows.Close()
	holders := map[string][]Assignment{}
	for rows.Next() {
		var role string
		var a Assignment
		if err := rows.Scan(&role, &a.Account, &a.Name, &a.Email, &a.Marketplace, &a.MarketplaceName, &a.AssignedAt); err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		holders[role] = append(holders[role], a)
	}
	return holders, rows.Err()
}

// Members returns every staff member, by name, for the assignment's form.
func (r *Roles) Members(ctx context.Context) ([]Member, error) {
	var members []Member
	err := r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `SELECT id::text, name, email FROM account WHERE kind = 'staff' ORDER BY lower(name), id`)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		members, err = pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowToStructByPos[Member])
		return err
	})
	return members, err
}

// Scopes returns every marketplace a role can be assigned in, by name.
func (r *Roles) Scopes(ctx context.Context) ([]Scope, error) {
	var scopes []Scope
	err := r.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `SELECT DISTINCT marketplace_id, name FROM tenancy_host_map() ORDER BY name, marketplace_id`)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		scopes, err = pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowToStructByPos[Scope])
		return err
	})
	return scopes, err
}
```

```go
// Package staff is who works on the platform and what each may do in the
// console (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md).
//
// Staff are accounts without a marketplace (internal/identity, on a platform
// visit). This package holds what is theirs alone: the owner and the first
// run that creates them, and the answer to whether a staff member may do
// something. Everything it reads or writes belongs to the platform, so it
// runs in transactions that name no marketplace.
package staff

import (
	"context"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
)

// Transactor opens a transaction that names no marketplace: the platform's
// (db.Pool.InTx).
type Transactor interface {
	InTx(ctx context.Context, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error
}

// Auditor appends to the audit log ((*audit.Log).Append).
type Auditor interface {
	Append(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, entry audit.Entry) error
}

// Platform is the scope of what belongs to no marketplace — the console's
// home, its build identifier — as opposed to one marketplace's id.
const Platform = ""

// Authoriser answers whether a staff account may do something in a scope:
// in one marketplace, named by its id, or across the platform (Platform).
// The console asks it for every page it guards and every entry of its menu,
// and answers "not found" when it says no (D8).
//
// Roles is the implementation: the owner may do everything, everywhere, and
// anybody else what the roles assigned to them grant in the marketplace
// asked about or across the platform (D3, D6, D7).
type Authoriser interface {
	Allows(ctx context.Context, account string, permission Permission, marketplace string) (bool, error)
}
```

```bash
git add -A cmd/marketplace docs/infrastructure.md internal/platform/httpx internal/staff migrations
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
Roles held by staff in a scope, and the owner role kept by the service and the database

The owner combines permissions into roles and assigns a role to a staff
member for one marketplace or the whole platform (F15 spec, D6); staff.Roles
makes and audits those changes, with the staff member as the actor, and is
the console's Authoriser in place of the owner-only one of the first pull
request: the owner may do every declared permission everywhere by their role
(D7), anybody else what a role grants where it is assigned (D3), and the
console's own permissions, its home and its build, wherever a role is
assigned, since the console's pages belong to every scope.

The owner role cannot be edited, deleted, assigned or stripped. The service
refuses it, and so does the database, to the application role writing SQL of
its own: one owner role, one assignment of it for the whole platform, a
foreign key that makes an assignment say truthfully whether it is the owner's,
and triggers that refuse to change the role, its permissions or its
assignment. A role is held by staff only, which the database now enforces
too (a gap the first pull request left to the code).
EOF
```

## Task 10: The console asks the roles: a permission per page, a marketplace per route

D3, D6 and D8: every console page asks for `console.view` and its own permission and is
"not found" without them; `/console/version` asks for `console.version` alone; the menu shows only what
may be opened. A page about one marketplace names it in its path; `forMarketplace` serves it only when a
role grants the permission there or across the platform, and only then may the handler open that
marketplace's transaction, through `tenantTx`, which refuses without the gate. No page of F15 shows a
marketplace's data, so the gate is proved on a page the tests mount (`export_test.go`) reading the real
`account` table with no filter of its own.

```go
// ownerID is the owner's account.
func (c console) ownerID(t *testing.T) string {
	t.Helper()
	var id string
	if err := c.pool.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT id::text FROM account WHERE email_normalised = $1`, ownerEmail).Scan(&id); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return id
}

// asOwner is the owner, as the actor of a change made through the service.
func (c console) asOwner(t *testing.T) staff.Actor {
	return staff.Actor{Account: c.ownerID(t), IP: "203.0.113.20", UserAgent: "test"}
}

// grant makes a role of permissions and assigns it to account in scope (a
// marketplace's id, or staff.Platform); it returns the role.
func (c console) grant(t *testing.T, account, scope string, permissions ...staff.Permission) string {
	t.Helper()
	existing, err := c.roles.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	role, err := c.roles.Create(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), fmt.Sprintf("Role %d", len(existing)), permissions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := c.roles.Assign(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), role, account, scope); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return role
}

// colleague creates a staff member who holds no role, signs them in on the
// console and has them add an app; it returns them, their account and the
// app's key.
func (c console) colleague(t *testing.T, email string) (*visitor, string, string) {
	t.Helper()
	var account string
	if _, err := c.service.CreateStaff(t.Context(), identity.Visit{Platform: true, Language: "pt-BR"}, "Colega", email,
		ownerPassword, func(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, id string) error { account = id; return nil }); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	v := c.onConsole(t)
	wantRedirect(t, v.signIn(email, ownerPassword), "/pt-BR/")
	return v, account, enrolAppOnConsole(t, v)
}

// through is the same visitor, cookies and all, sending its requests to
// another handler.
func (v *visitor) through(handler http.Handler) *visitor {
	return &visitor{t: v.t, handler: handler, place: v.place, cookies: v.cookies}
}
```

```go
// Each console page asks for its own permission, and every page but the
// build's for the console's view first; a role assigned for one marketplace
// opens the console as one for the whole platform does, since the console's
// own pages belong to every scope (D6, D8).
func TestEachConsolePageAsksForItsPermission(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	viewer, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "colega@example.test")
	role := c.grant(t, account, staff.Platform, staff.ConsoleView)
	home := viewer.get("/")
	if home.Code != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(home.Body.String(), `href="/pt-BR/"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the home with console.view: status %d, body %s", home.Code, home.Body.String())
	}
	if answer := viewer.get("/console/version"); answer.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("the version without console.version = %d, want 404", answer.Code)
	}
	if err := c.roles.Edit(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), role, "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.ConsoleVersion}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if answer := viewer.get("/console/version"); answer.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("the version with console.version = %d, want 200", answer.Code)
	}

	local, localAccount, _ := c.colleague(t, "local@example.test")
	c.grant(t, localAccount, c.marketplace.ID, staff.ConsoleView)
	if answer := local.get("/"); answer.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("the home for a staff member of one marketplace = %d, want 200", answer.Code)
	}

	builds, buildsAccount, _ := c.colleague(t, "builds@example.test")
	c.grant(t, buildsAccount, staff.Platform, staff.ConsoleVersion)
	if answer := builds.get("/"); answer.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("the home without console.view = %d, want 404", answer.Code)
	}
	if answer := builds.get("/console/version"); answer.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("the version with console.version alone = %d, want 200", answer.Code)
	}
}

// A console page about one marketplace names it, and is served only to a
// staff member whose roles grant its permission there or across the
// platform, reading under that marketplace's row-level security. The page
// here reads every buyer's name with no filter of its own, as a page that
// forgot one would: a staff member of one marketplace reads that
// marketplace's buyers and cannot reach the other's; the owner, and a staff
// member of the whole platform, read each marketplace's own (D3, D8).
func TestAStaffMemberOfOneMarketplaceCannotReadAnother(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	confirmedAccount(t, c.pool, c.service, c.marketplace, "Leitora", "leitora@example.test", ownerPassword)
	confirmedAccount(t, c.pool, c.service, c.other, "Comprador", "comprador@example.test", ownerPassword)
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.Handle("GET /marketplaces/{marketplace}", c.site.MarketplacePage(staff.ConsoleView,
		func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx) (string, error) {
			var names string
			err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT coalesce(string_agg(name, ',' ORDER BY name), '') FROM account`).Scan(&names)
			return names, err
		}))
	page := httpx.Sessions(c.service, silent())(mux)
	read := func(v *visitor, marketplace string) *httptest.ResponseRecorder {
		return v.through(page).get("/marketplaces/" + marketplace)
	}

	local, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "local@example.test")
	c.grant(t, account, c.marketplace.ID, staff.ConsoleView)
	if own := read(local, c.marketplace.ID); own.Code != http.StatusOK || own.Body.String() != "Leitora" {
		t.Fatalf("their own marketplace: status %d, body %q; want Leitora", own.Code, own.Body.String())
	}
	for _, elsewhere := range []string{c.other.ID, "not-a-marketplace"} {
		if answer := read(local, elsewhere); answer.Code != http.StatusNotFound || strings.Contains(answer.Body.String(), "Comprador") {
			t.Fatalf("%s for a staff member of another: status %d, body %q; want 404", elsewhere, answer.Code, answer.Body.String())
		}
	}

	platform, platformAccount, _ := c.colleague(t, "platform@example.test")
	c.grant(t, platformAccount, staff.Platform, staff.ConsoleView)
	owner := c.onConsole(t)
	wantRedirect(t, owner.signIn(ownerEmail, ownerPassword), "/pt-BR/")
	enrolAppOnConsole(t, owner)
	for _, v := range []*visitor{platform, owner} {
		if other := read(v, c.other.ID); other.Code != http.StatusOK || other.Body.String() != "Comprador" {
			t.Fatalf("the other marketplace across the platform: status %d, body %q; want Comprador", other.Code, other.Body.String())
		}
	}
}
```

```go
// MarketplacePage is a console page about one marketplace, behind its gate
// (forMarketplace): it answers what read finds in that marketplace's
// transaction. No page of F15 shows a marketplace's data — its pages are the
// platform's — so the tests mount this one to prove the gate that the first
// such page will stand behind (F15 spec, D3).
func (s Site) MarketplacePage(permission staff.Permission, read func(context.Context, pgx.Tx) (string, error)) http.Handler {
	return staffOnly(withFactor(s.forMarketplace(permission, http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		var found string
		if err := s.tenantTx(r, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
			var err error
			found, err = read(r.Context(), tx)
			return err
		}); err != nil {
			http.Error(w, "", http.StatusInternalServerError)
			return
		}
		_, _ = io.WriteString(w, found)
	}))))
}

// MarketplaceTx exposes tenantTx, to show it refuses a request the gate
// did not pass.
func (s Site) MarketplaceTx(r *http.Request, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error {
	return s.tenantTx(r, fn)
}
```

Create `internal/platform/httpx/scope_test.go`:

```go
package httpx_test

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"io"
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity/breached"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/httpx"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/i18n"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/ratelimit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
)

const (
	marketplaceA = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000a"
	marketplaceB = "00000000-0000-0000-0000-00000000000b"
)

// grants is an Authoriser that allows what it lists, as "permission@scope",
// and fails for the scope failing names.
type grants struct {
	allowed []string
	failing string
}

func (g grants) Allows(_ context.Context, _ string, permission staff.Permission, marketplace string) (bool, error) {
	if marketplace != staff.Platform && marketplace == g.failing {
		return false, errors.New("the database did not answer")
	}
	return slices.Contains(g.allowed, string(permission)+"@"+marketplace), nil
}

// opening records the marketplaces whose transactions were opened.
type opening struct{ opened []string }

func (o *opening) InTxFor(_ context.Context, marketplace string, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error {
	o.opened = append(o.opened, marketplace)
	return fn(nil)
}

// marketplacePage is a console page about one marketplace, behind the gate,
// answering "read" once its transaction is open; and the transactions it
// opened.
func marketplacePage(t *testing.T, g grants, permission staff.Permission) (http.Handler, *opening) {
	t.Helper()
	catalogue, err := i18n.Load()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	never := ratelimit.Never{}
	tenants := &opening{}
	site := httpx.NewSite(nil, catalogue, nil, false).WithIdentity(httpx.IdentityRoutes{
		Service: identity.NewService(nil, identity.NewHasher(cheap, 1), breached.Fake{}, nil, silent()),
		Pages:   httpx.NewPages(catalogue), Log: silent(),
		Limits: httpx.IdentityLimits{SignUp: never, Resend: never, ResendAddress: never, SignIn: never,
			SignInAddress: never, Password: never, StepUp: never},
	}).WithConsole(httpx.ConsoleRoutes{Authoriser: g, Tenants: tenants})
	mux := http.NewServeMux()
	mux.Handle("GET /marketplaces/{marketplace}", site.MarketplacePage(permission,
		func(context.Context, pgx.Tx) (string, error) { return "read", nil }))
	return mux, tenants
}

// asStaff is a request on the console by a staff member with a second
// factor, or by nobody when signedIn is false.
func asStaff(t *testing.T, path string, signedIn bool) *http.Request {
	request := onConsole(httptest.NewRequestWithContext(t.Context(), http.MethodGet, path, nil))
	if signedIn {
		request = request.WithContext(httpx.WithSession(request.Context(), identity.Session{
			ID: "session", SecondFactor: true, Account: identity.Account{ID: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000001"}}))
	}
	return request
}

// A console page about a marketplace names it, and opens that marketplace's
// transaction only for a staff member whose roles grant the console's view
// and the page's permission there — "not found" to anybody else, and when
// the answer cannot be had, before any transaction is opened (D3, D8).
func TestAMarketplacePageOpensOnlyThatMarketplace(t *testing.T) {
	everywhere := []string{"console.view@", "staff.invite@" + marketplaceA}
	for _, c := range []struct {
		name     string
		grants   grants
		path     string
		signedIn bool
		status   int
		opened   []string
	}{
		{"granted there", grants{allowed: everywhere}, "/marketplaces/" + marketplaceA, true, http.StatusOK, []string{marketplaceA}},
		{"granted elsewhere", grants{allowed: everywhere}, "/marketplaces/" + marketplaceB, true, http.StatusNotFound, nil},
		{"not a marketplace", grants{allowed: everywhere}, "/marketplaces/a", true, http.StatusNotFound, nil},
		{"no console", grants{allowed: []string{"staff.invite@" + marketplaceA}}, "/marketplaces/" + marketplaceA, true, http.StatusNotFound, nil},
		{"no answer", grants{allowed: everywhere, failing: marketplaceA}, "/marketplaces/" + marketplaceA, true, http.StatusNotFound, nil},
		{"signed out", grants{allowed: everywhere}, "/marketplaces/" + marketplaceA, false, http.StatusSeeOther, nil},
	} {
		t.Run(c.name, func(t *testing.T) {
			page, tenants := marketplacePage(t, c.grants, staff.StaffInvite)
			recorder := httptest.NewRecorder()
			page.ServeHTTP(recorder, asStaff(t, c.path, c.signedIn))
			if recorder.Code != c.status || !slices.Equal(tenants.opened, c.opened) {
				t.Fatalf("status %d having opened %v, want %d having opened %v", recorder.Code, tenants.opened, c.status, c.opened)
			}
			if c.status == http.StatusOK {
				if body, _ := io.ReadAll(recorder.Body); string(body) != "read" {
					t.Fatalf("the page answered %q", body)
				}
			}
		})
	}
}

// A handler that was not given a marketplace by the gate cannot open one:
// there is no way to a tenant's transaction on the console but through it.
func TestAMarketplaceTransactionNeedsTheGate(t *testing.T) {
	catalogue, err := i18n.Load()
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tenants := &opening{}
	site := httpx.NewSite(nil, catalogue, nil, false).WithConsole(httpx.ConsoleRoutes{Tenants: tenants})
	called := false
	err = site.MarketplaceTx(asStaff(t, "/marketplaces/"+marketplaceA, true), func(pgx.Tx) error { called = true; return nil })
	if err == nil || called || len(tenants.opened) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("an ungated transaction = %v, called %v, opened %v; want refused", err, called, tenants.opened)
	}
}
```

```go
// scopeKey carries the marketplace the gate allowed a console page to read.
type scopeKey struct{}

// errUngated is a console handler asking for a marketplace's transaction
// without the gate having named one: a mistake in the code, refused rather
// than served.
var errUngated = errors.New("httpx: a marketplace's transaction was asked for outside its gate")

// forMarketplace is the gate of a console page about one marketplace (F15
// spec, D3): the page names the marketplace in its path, as {marketplace},
// and next is served only when the staff member's roles grant the console's
// view and permission in that marketplace or across the platform — "not
// found" otherwise, before anything of the marketplace's is read (D8).
// next then reads it through tenantTx, under that marketplace's
// row-level security, so a query that forgot its filter still sees no other
// marketplace's rows.
func (s Site) forMarketplace(permission staff.Permission, next http.Handler) http.Handler {
	return http.HandlerFunc(func(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
		marketplace := r.PathValue("marketplace")
		if marketplace == staff.Platform || !s.mayOpen(r, permission, marketplace) {
			http.NotFound(w, r)
			return
		}
		next.ServeHTTP(w, r.WithContext(context.WithValue(r.Context(), scopeKey{}, marketplace)))
	})
}

// tenantTx runs fn in the transaction of the marketplace the gate
// allowed this request, and refuses when there is none: on the console, a
// tenant's rows are reached through the gate or not at all.
func (s Site) tenantTx(r *http.Request, fn func(pgx.Tx) error) error {
	marketplace, _ := r.Context().Value(scopeKey{}).(string)
	if marketplace == "" || s.console == nil || s.console.Tenants == nil {
		return errUngated
	}
	return s.console.Tenants.InTxFor(r.Context(), marketplace, fn)
}
```

## Task 11: The role pages: roles listed, made, edited, assigned, removed and deleted, behind a step-up

The spec's flow "Roles" and D10: the list (the owner role first, marked as protected), a
role's page with its permissions and holders, the forms to make and edit a role, to assign it for one
marketplace or the whole platform, to remove a holder and to delete a role nobody holds. Every page asks
for `console.view` and `roles.manage` across the platform. Every change asks for the second factor again
first, through F14's step-up with a new action for changes to the platform's staff (`identity.ActionStaff`,
which PR 3 reuses); the form pages ask before they are shown, so a post is not lost. A refusal is said on
the field it is about (422); a change sent for the owner role is refused on its page (409).

**Files:**
- Create: `web/roles.go`, `web/roles.templ`, `internal/platform/httpx/roles.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/roles_integration_test.go`
- Modify: `internal/identity/factorpolicy.go`, `internal/identity/factorpolicy_test.go`, `internal/identity/stepup.go`, `web/layout.templ`, `web/locales/en-US.json`, `web/locales/pt-BR.json`, `internal/platform/httpx/console.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/console_integration_test.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/console_test.go`, `cmd/marketplace/main.go`
- Regenerated by `make generate` (committed): the `*_templ.go` of every template this task touches

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `staff.Roles` and its errors (task 9); `permitted`, `consoleMenu` (task 10);
  F14's `NeedsStepUp`, `toStepUp`, `identity.Policy`, `identity.ParseAction`.
- Produces: `identity.ActionStaff Action = "staff"`; `ConsoleRoutes.Roles *staff.Roles`; private
  `rolesPath`, `(s Site) roleRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, secured func(http.Handler) http.Handler)`, `roleDone`,
  `(s Site) stepUpFirst(w, r, back string) bool`, `actor(r *http.Request) staff.Actor`,
  `roleRefusal(err error) (web.Form, int, bool)`, `roleView`, `roleForm`, `posted`, `toRole`, `roleChanged`,
  `showRole`, `refuseOnRole`; `web.RoleView`, `web.Holder`, `web.Roles`, `web.Choice`, `web.Option`,

```go
// recorded is an auditor that keeps what it is given.
type recorded struct {
	mu      sync.Mutex
	entries []audit.Entry
}

func (r *recorded) Append(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, e audit.Entry) error {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	r.entries = append(r.entries, e)
	return nil
}

// find is the last entry of action, if there is one.
func (r *recorded) find(action string) (audit.Entry, bool) {
	r.mu.Lock()
	defer r.mu.Unlock()
	for i := len(r.entries) - 1; i >= 0; i-- {
		if r.entries[i].Action == action {
			return r.entries[i], true
		}
	}
	return audit.Entry{}, false
}

```

Create `internal/platform/httpx/roles_integration_test.go`:

```go
//go:build integration

package httpx_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/url"
	"regexp"
	"strings"
	"testing"
	"time"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
)

// stepUpOnConsole answers the step-up a change to the staff asks for, with
// the app's code of the next step, and checks it returns to back.
func stepUpOnConsole(t *testing.T, v *visitor, key, back string) {
	t.Helper()
	page := v.get("/account/verify?for=staff&next=" + url.QueryEscape(back))
	if page.Code != http.StatusOK {
		t.Fatalf("the step-up page: status %d, body %s", page.Code, page.Body.String())
	}
	wantRedirect(t, v.post("/account/verify", url.Values{"method": {"totp"}, "for": {"staff"}, "next": {back},
		"code": {appCodeFor(t, key, time.Now().Add(30*time.Second))}}), "/pt-BR"+back)
}

// signedInOwner is the owner, signed in on the console with an app, and the
// app's key.
func (c console) signedInOwner(t *testing.T) (*visitor, string) {
	t.Helper()
	v := c.onConsole(t)
	wantRedirect(t, v.signIn(ownerEmail, ownerPassword), "/pt-BR/")
	return v, enrolAppOnConsole(t, v)
}

// createdRole reads the role a creation was redirected to.
var createdRole = regexp.MustCompile(`^/pt-BR/roles/([0-9a-f-]{36})\?done=created$`)

// wantStatus fails the test unless the page answered status and says what
// the key of the message says in pt-BR.
func wantStatus(t *testing.T, page *httptest.ResponseRecorder, status int, says string) {
	t.Helper()
	if page.Code != status || !strings.Contains(page.Body.String(), says) {
		t.Fatalf("status %d, want %d saying %q; body %s", page.Code, status, says, page.Body.String())
	}
}

// The owner manages the roles from the console: the list shows the owner
// role as protected; creating, editing, assigning, removing and deleting a
// role each ask for the second factor again first, say what went wrong on
// the field it is about, and are audited with the owner as the actor; a
// role somebody holds is not deleted; and the owner role's page offers no
// change, and refuses one sent anyway (D6, D7, D10).
func TestTheOwnerManagesRolesFromTheConsole(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	owner, key := c.signedInOwner(t)
	colleague, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "colega@example.test")

	list := owner.get("/roles")
	body := list.Body.String()
	if list.Code != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(body, `class="protected"`) || !strings.Contains(body, "Proprietário") ||
		!strings.Contains(body, `href="/pt-BR/roles" aria-current="page"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the roles: status %d, body %s", list.Code, body)
	}

	// A change asks for the second factor again first.
	wantRedirect(t, owner.get("/roles/new"), "/pt-BR/account/verify?for=staff&next=%2Froles%2Fnew")
	stepUpOnConsole(t, owner, key, "/roles/new")
	form := owner.get("/roles/new")
	if form.Code != http.StatusOK || strings.Count(form.Body.String(), `type="checkbox" name="permission"`) != len(staff.Permissions()) {
		t.Fatalf("the new role's form: status %d, body %s", form.Code, form.Body.String())
	}
	unnamed := owner.post("/roles", url.Values{"name": {" "}, "permission": {"console.view"}})
	wantStatus(t, unnamed, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Dê um nome ao papel.")
	if !strings.Contains(input(t, unnamed.Body.String(), "name"), `aria-invalid="true"`) ||
		!strings.Contains(unnamed.Body.String(), `value="console.view" checked`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refusal is not on the name, or lost the permissions: %s", unnamed.Body.String())
	}
	created := owner.post("/roles", url.Values{"name": {"Suporte"}, "permission": {"console.view", "staff.invite"}})
	found := createdRole.FindStringSubmatch(created.Header().Get("Location"))
	if created.Code != http.StatusSeeOther || found == nil {
		t.Fatalf("creating a role: status %d to %q", created.Code, created.Header().Get("Location"))
	}
	role := found[1]
	if entry, ok := c.trail.find("staff.role_created"); !ok || entry.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: c.ownerID(t), Kind: audit.Staff}) ||
		entry.Subject.ID != role || entry.From != "203.0.113.20" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_created = %+v (found %v)", entry, ok)
	}
	page := owner.get("/roles/" + role + "?done=created")
	wantStatus(t, page, http.StatusOK, "O papel foi criado.")
	if !strings.Contains(page.Body.String(), `value="staff.invite" checked`) || strings.Contains(page.Body.String(), `value="roles.manage" checked`) {
		t.Fatalf("the role's page does not show its permissions: %s", page.Body.String())
	}

	wantRedirect(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role, url.Values{"name": {"Atendimento"}, "permission": {"console.view"}}),
		"/pt-BR/roles/"+role+"?done=edited")
	wantStatus(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role, url.Values{"name": {"owner"}}), http.StatusUnprocessableEntity,
		"Outro papel já tem este nome.")

	// Assigned for one marketplace, it opens the console there and nothing
	// more.
	wantStatus(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role+"/assign", url.Values{"account": {"not-an-account"}, "scope": {""}}),
		http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Escolha um membro da equipe.")
	wantRedirect(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role+"/assign", url.Values{"account": {account}, "scope": {c.marketplace.ID}}),
		"/pt-BR/roles/"+role+"?done=assigned")
	if entry, ok := c.trail.find("staff.role_assigned"); !ok || entry.Subject.ID != account || entry.Actor.ID != c.ownerID(t) {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_assigned = %+v (found %v)", entry, ok)
	}
	held := owner.get("/roles/" + role)
	if !strings.Contains(held.Body.String(), "Colega") || !strings.Contains(held.Body.String(), "Em: Loja") {
		t.Fatalf("the role's page does not show who holds it where: %s", held.Body.String())
	}
	home := colleague.get("/")
	if home.Code != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(home.Body.String(), `href="/pt-BR/roles"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague's home: status %d, body %s", home.Code, home.Body.String())
	}
	if roles := colleague.get("/roles"); roles.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("the roles for a colleague who may not manage them = %d, want 404", roles.Code)
	}

	wantStatus(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role+"/delete", nil), http.StatusUnprocessableEntity,
		"Um papel que alguém tem não pode ser excluído.")
	wantRedirect(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role+"/unassign", url.Values{"account": {account}, "scope": {c.marketplace.ID}}),
		"/pt-BR/roles/"+role+"?done=unassigned")
	if roles := colleague.get("/"); roles.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague's home once the role is removed = %d, want 404", roles.Code)
	}
	wantRedirect(t, owner.post("/roles/"+role+"/delete", nil), "/pt-BR/roles?done=deleted")
	if gone := owner.get("/roles/" + role); gone.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
		t.Fatalf("a deleted role's page = %d, want 404", gone.Code)
	}
	if entry, ok := c.trail.find("staff.role_deleted"); !ok || entry.Subject.ID != role {
		t.Fatalf("staff.role_deleted = %+v (found %v)", entry, ok)
	}

	// The owner role: shown, never changed.
	roles, err := c.roles.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	ownerRole := roles[0].ID
	protected := owner.get("/roles/" + ownerRole)
	if protected.Code != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(protected.Body.String(), `class="protected"`) ||
		strings.Contains(protected.Body.String(), `action="/pt-BR/roles/`) {
		t.Fatalf("the owner role's page: status %d, body %s", protected.Code, protected.Body.String())
	}
	for path, form := range map[string]url.Values{
		"":          {"name": {"Mine"}},
		"/delete":   nil,
		"/assign":   {"account": {account}, "scope": {""}},
		"/unassign": {"account": {c.ownerID(t)}, "scope": {""}},
	} {
		wantStatus(t, owner.post("/roles/"+ownerRole+path, form), http.StatusConflict,
			"O papel de proprietário não pode ser alterado.")
	}
}

// The role pages are "not found" to a staff member who may not manage roles
// across the platform, and a change they send changes nothing (D8).
func TestTheRolesAreNotFoundWithoutTheirPermission(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	v, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "colega@example.test")
	c.grant(t, account, staff.Platform, staff.ConsoleView)
	c.grant(t, account, c.marketplace.ID, staff.RolesManage)
	for _, answer := range []*httptest.ResponseRecorder{
		v.get("/roles"), v.get("/roles/new"),
		v.post("/roles", url.Values{"name": {"Mine"}, "permission": {"roles.manage"}}),
	} {
		if answer.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("a role page for a staff member who may not manage roles = %d, want 404", answer.Code)
		}
	}
	if roles, err := c.roles.List(t.Context()); err != nil || len(roles) != 3 {
		t.Fatalf("there are %d roles (%v), want the owner's and the two granted", len(roles), err)
	}
}
```

Create `web/roles.go`:

```go
package web

// The role pages of the console (F15 spec, Flows "Roles"). A role's name,
// a staff member's name and address and a marketplace's name are people's
// words, shown as they were typed; everything else goes through a key.

// RoleView is a role as the pages show it. Owner marks the owner role, which
// is named by a key in the reader's language rather than by its stored name,
// and holds every permission without listing one. Permissions are the keys of
// the labels of what the role grants.
type RoleView struct {
	ID          string
	Name        string
	Owner       bool
	Permissions []string
	Holders     []Holder
}

// Holder is a staff member holding a role in a scope: Where is the
// marketplace's name, or empty for the whole platform, and Scope its id,
// which removing the assignment sends back.
type Holder struct {
	Account string
	Name    string
	Email   string
	Scope   string
	Where   string
}

// Roles is the list of roles, and what was just done.
type Roles struct {
	Roles []RoleView
	Done  string
}

// Choice is one permission on a role's form: the key sent, the key of its
// label, and whether it is ticked.
type Choice struct {
	Value   string
	Label   string
	Checked bool
}

// Option is one entry of a list to choose from: a staff member, by id,
// name and address, or a marketplace, by id and name.
type Option struct {
	Value  string
	Label  string
	Detail string
}

// RoleForm is a role's name and permissions, as a form shows them: the
// role's own, or what was typed and refused. NameMax is the name's bound,
// which comes from internal/staff.
type RoleForm struct {
	Name    string
	Choices []Choice
	NameMax int
	Form    Form
}

// RolePage is one role: its form, who holds it where, and what it can be
// assigned to; what was just done, and a refusal of anything but the form.
type RolePage struct {
	Role    RoleView
	Edit    RoleForm
	Members []Option
	Scopes  []Option
	Done    string
	Form    Form
}

// OwnerName is what the owner role is called on the pages, in the reader's
// language.
const OwnerName = "console.roles.owner"

// RoleName is what a role is called on a page.
func RoleName(page Page, role RoleView) string {
	if role.Owner {
		return page.T(OwnerName)
	}
	return role.Name
}

// Where is a holder's scope as a page says it.
func Where(page Page, holder Holder) string {
	if holder.Scope == "" {
		return page.T("console.roles.everywhere")
	}
	return holder.Where
}
```

```templ
		   protected by a word, not a colour alone; its holders one per

		.protected {
			background: var(--notice-bg);

			color: var(--notice-text);

		ul.roles a:active, main > p > a:active { background: var(--pressed-tint); }
		ul.holders li { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.25rem 1rem; align-items: baseline; margin-top: 0.5rem; }

			margin: 0;
			padding: 0;
			width: 1.125rem;
		}
		main form select {
			border: 1px solid var(--field-edge);
			border-radius: 0.375rem;
			box-sizing: border-box;
			display: block;
			font: inherit;
			margin-top: 0.25rem;
			max-width: 24rem;

			width: 100%;
		}
		main form select[aria-invalid="true"] { border-color: var(--error-edge); box-shadow: 0 0 0 1px var(--error-edge); }
	</style>
}
```
