Create `web/roles.templ`:

```templ
package web

import "strconv"

// RolesList is every role, the owner role first and marked as protected,
// each with what it grants and who holds it where (F15 spec, Flows "Roles").
templ RolesList(page Page, view Roles) {
	@Layout(page, page.T("console.roles.title")) {
		<h1>{ page.T("console.roles.title") }</h1>
		if view.Done != "" {
			<p role="status">{ page.T(view.Done) }</p>
		}
		<p>{ page.T("console.roles.intro") }</p>
		<p><a href={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/new") }>{ page.T("console.roles.new") }</a></p>
		<ul class="roles">
			for _, role := range view.Roles {
				<li>
					<h2>
						<a href={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/" + role.ID) }>{ RoleName(page, role) }</a>
						if role.Owner {
							<span class="protected">{ page.T("console.roles.protected") }</span>
						}
					</h2>
					@grants(page, role)
					@holders(page, role, false)
				</li>
			}
		</ul>
	}
}

// grants is what a role allows: every permission for the owner role, which
// is said rather than listed, and the labels of the others'.
templ grants(page Page, role RoleView) {
	if role.Owner {
		<p>{ page.T("console.roles.owner_intro") }</p>
	} else if len(role.Permissions) == 0 {
		<p>{ page.T("console.roles.no_permissions") }</p>
	} else {
		<ul class="permissions">
			for _, label := range role.Permissions {
				<li>{ page.T(label) }</li>
			}
		</ul>
	}
}

// holders is who holds a role and where, each with the way to remove it
// when removable: never for the owner role (D7).
templ holders(page Page, role RoleView, removable bool) {
	if len(role.Holders) == 0 {
		<p>{ page.T("console.roles.no_holders") }</p>
	} else {
		<ul class="holders">
			for _, holder := range role.Holders {
				<li>
					<strong>{ holder.Name }</strong>
					<span>{ holder.Email }</span>
					<span>{ page.T("console.roles.in", Where(page, holder)) }</span>
					if removable {
						<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/" + role.ID + "/unassign") }>
							<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
							<input type="hidden" name="account" value={ holder.Account }/>
							<input type="hidden" name="scope" value={ holder.Scope }/>
							<button
								type="submit"
								class="secondary"
								aria-label={ page.T("console.roles.remove_named", holder.Name, Where(page, holder)) }
							>{ page.T("console.roles.remove") }</button>
						</form>
					}
				</li>
			}
		</ul>
	}
}

// roleFields are a role's name and its permissions, one choice each, in the
// registry's order.
templ roleFields(page Page, form RoleForm) {
	<label>
		{ page.T("console.roles.name") }
		<input
			name="name"
			required
			maxlength={ strconv.Itoa(form.NameMax) }
			value={ form.Name }
			{ form.Form.Input("name", "name-hint")... }
		/>
	</label>
	<p id="name-hint" class="hint">{ page.T("console.roles.name_hint", form.NameMax) }</p>
	<fieldset class="choices">
		<legend>{ page.T("console.roles.permissions") }</legend>
		for _, choice := range form.Choices {
			<label class="choice">
				<input type="checkbox" name="permission" value={ choice.Value } checked?={ choice.Checked }/>
				<span>{ page.T(choice.Label) }</span>
			</label>
		}
	</fieldset>
}

// refusal is a page's one refusal, which the field it is about points at.
templ refusal(form Form, page Page) {
	if form.Error != "" {
		<p id={ ErrorID } role="alert">{ page.T(form.Error, form.ErrorArgs...) }</p>
	}
}

// NewRole is the form that makes a role.
templ NewRole(page Page, form RoleForm) {
	@Layout(page, page.T("console.roles.new")) {
		<h1>{ page.T("console.roles.new") }</h1>
		@refusal(form.Form, page)
		<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles") }>
			<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
			@roleFields(page, form)
			<button type="submit">{ page.T("console.roles.create") }</button>
		</form>
		<p><a href={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles") }>{ page.T("console.roles.back") }</a></p>
	}
}

// Role is one role's page: its form, who holds it where with the way to
// remove each, the way to assign it, and the way to delete it while nobody
// holds it. The owner role's page shows what it is and who holds it, and
// offers no change (D7).
templ Role(page Page, view RolePage) {
	@Layout(page, RoleName(page, view.Role)) {
		<h1>
			{ RoleName(page, view.Role) }
			if view.Role.Owner {
				<span class="protected">{ page.T("console.roles.protected") }</span>
			}
		</h1>
		if view.Done != "" {
			<p role="status">{ page.T(view.Done) }</p>
		}
		if view.Edit.Form.Error != "" {
			@refusal(view.Edit.Form, page)
		} else {
			@refusal(view.Form, page)
		}
		if view.Role.Owner {
			@grants(page, view.Role)
			<h2>{ page.T("console.roles.holders") }</h2>
			@holders(page, view.Role, false)
		} else {
			<h2>{ page.T("console.roles.edit") }</h2>
			<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/" + view.Role.ID) }>
				<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
				@roleFields(page, view.Edit)
				<button type="submit">{ page.T("console.roles.save") }</button>
			</form>
			<h2>{ page.T("console.roles.holders") }</h2>
			@holders(page, view.Role, true)
			<h2>{ page.T("console.roles.assign_title") }</h2>
			<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/" + view.Role.ID + "/assign") }>
				<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
				<label>
					{ page.T("console.roles.member") }
					<select name="account" required { view.Form.Input("account")... }>
						for _, member := range view.Members {
							<option value={ member.Value }>{ page.T("console.roles.member_option", member.Label, member.Detail) }</option>
						}
					</select>
				</label>
				<label>
					{ page.T("console.roles.scope") }
					<select name="scope" { view.Form.Input("scope")... }>
						<option value="">{ page.T("console.roles.everywhere") }</option>
						for _, scope := range view.Scopes {
							<option value={ scope.Value }>{ scope.Label }</option>
						}
					</select>
				</label>
				<button type="submit">{ page.T("console.roles.assign") }</button>
			</form>
			<h2>{ page.T("console.roles.delete_title") }</h2>
			if len(view.Role.Holders) > 0 {
				<p>{ page.T("console.roles.error.held") }</p>
			} else {
				<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles/" + view.Role.ID + "/delete") }>
					<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
					<button type="submit" class="secondary">{ page.T("console.roles.delete") }</button>
				</form>
			}
		}
		<p><a href={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/roles") }>{ page.T("console.roles.back") }</a></p>
	}
}
```

Create `internal/platform/httpx/roles.go`:

```go
package httpx

import (
	"errors"
	"net/http"
	"slices"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/i18n"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/web"
)

// The role pages of the console (F15 spec, Flows "Roles"): the roles with
// what each grants and who holds it where; a role made, edited, assigned,
// removed and deleted. Every page asks for the console's view and
// roles.manage across the platform, and is "not found" without them (D8);
// every change asks for the second factor again first (D10), and is audited
// with the staff member as its actor (staff.Roles).

// rolesPath is the roles' list; a role's page is below it, by id.
const rolesPath = "/roles"

// roleRoutes mounts the role pages, behind secured and the permission.
func (s Site) roleRoutes(mux *http.ServeMux, secured func(http.Handler) http.Handler) {
	if s.console.Roles == nil {
		return
	}
	guarded := func(h http.HandlerFunc) http.Handler { return secured(s.permitted(staff.RolesManage, h)) }
	mux.Handle("GET "+rolesPath, guarded(s.rolesPage))
	mux.Handle("GET "+rolesPath+"/new", guarded(s.newRoleForm))
	mux.Handle("POST "+rolesPath, guarded(s.createRole))
	mux.Handle("GET "+rolesPath+"/{role}", guarded(s.rolePage))
	mux.Handle("POST "+rolesPath+"/{role}", guarded(s.editRole))
	mux.Handle("POST "+rolesPath+"/{role}/assign", guarded(s.assignRole))
	mux.Handle("POST "+rolesPath+"/{role}/unassign", guarded(s.unassignRole))
	mux.Handle("POST "+rolesPath+"/{role}/delete", guarded(s.deleteRole))
}

// roleDone is what a redirect back to a role page may say was done: the
// query names it, and only these keys are shown.
var roleDone = map[string]string{
	"created":    "console.roles.done.created",
	"edited":     "console.roles.done.edited",
	"deleted":    "console.roles.done.deleted",
	"assigned":   "console.roles.done.assigned",
	"unassigned": "console.roles.done.unassigned",
}

// stepUpFirst sends a staff member who has not proved their second factor
// in the last ten minutes to prove it, to come back to back, and reports
// whether it did (D10). A change posted without it is not made: the form is
// shown again after the step-up.
func (s Site) stepUpFirst(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, back string) bool {
	session, _ := SessionFrom(r.Context())
	if !s.identity.Service.NeedsStepUp(session, identity.ActionStaff) {
		return false
	}
	toStepUp(w, r, identity.ActionStaff, back)
	return true
}

// actor is the signed-in staff member, as the audit records who made a
// change and from where.
func actor(r *http.Request) staff.Actor {
	session, _ := SessionFrom(r.Context())
	v := visit(r)
	return staff.Actor{Account: session.Account.ID, IP: v.IP, UserAgent: v.UserAgent}
}

// roleRefusal is how a page says the roles refused a change: the key of the
// message, the field it is about, and the status; false for an error that is
// not a refusal.
func roleRefusal(err error) (web.Form, int, bool) {
	refused := func(key, field string, args ...any) (web.Form, int, bool) {
		return web.Form{Error: key, ErrorArgs: args, Field: field}, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, true
	}
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNameMissing):
		return refused("console.roles.error.name_missing", "name")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNameInvalid):
		return refused("console.roles.error.name_invalid", "name", staff.MaxRoleNameLength)
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNameTaken):
		return refused("console.roles.error.name_taken", "name")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrPermissionUnknown):
		return refused("console.roles.error.permission_unknown", "")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleHeld):
		return refused("console.roles.error.held", "")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotStaff):
		return refused("console.roles.error.not_staff", "account")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrScopeUnknown):
		return refused("console.roles.error.scope_unknown", "scope")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrAlreadyAssigned):
		return refused("console.roles.error.already_assigned", "")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAssigned):
		return refused("console.roles.error.not_assigned", "")
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnerRole):
		return web.Form{Error: "console.roles.error.owner"}, http.StatusConflict, true
	}
	return web.Form{}, 0, false
}

// roleView is a role as the pages show it.
func roleView(role staff.Role) web.RoleView {
	view := web.RoleView{ID: role.ID, Name: role.Name, Owner: role.Owner}
	for _, p := range role.Permissions {
		view.Permissions = append(view.Permissions, p.Label())
	}
	for _, h := range role.Holders {
		view.Holders = append(view.Holders, web.Holder{Account: h.Account, Name: h.Name, Email: h.Email,
			Scope: h.Marketplace, Where: h.MarketplaceName})
	}
	return view
}

// roleForm is a role's form: its name and every declared permission, ticked
// where held.
func roleForm(name string, held []staff.Permission, form web.Form) web.RoleForm {
	view := web.RoleForm{Name: name, NameMax: staff.MaxRoleNameLength, Form: form}
	for _, p := range staff.Permissions() {
		view.Choices = append(view.Choices, web.Choice{Value: string(p), Label: p.Label(), Checked: slices.Contains(held, p)})
	}
	return view
}

// posted is the name and the permissions a role's form sent.
func posted(r *http.Request) (string, []staff.Permission) {
	_ = r.ParseForm()
	var permissions []staff.Permission
	for _, value := range r.PostForm["permission"] {
		permissions = append(permissions, staff.Permission(value))
	}
	return r.PostFormValue("name"), permissions
}

// toRole sends the staff member to a role's page, saying what was done. It
// is only called once the roles found role, so role is a role's id.
func toRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, role, done string) {
	// #nosec G710 -- a path on this site, never a host: role is the id of a role the
	// roles service has just found or made, and done one of roleDone's keys.
	http.Redirect(w, r, "/"+i18n.FromContext(r.Context())+rolesPath+"/"+role+"?done="+done, http.StatusSeeOther)
}

func (s Site) rolesPage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	roles, err := s.console.Roles.List(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "the roles could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	view := web.Roles{Done: roleDone[r.URL.Query().Get("done")]}
	for _, role := range roles {
		view.Roles = append(view.Roles, roleView(role))
	}
	render(w, r, web.RolesList(s.page(r, rolesPath), view))
}

func (s Site) newRoleForm(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/new") {
		return
	}
	render(w, r, web.NewRole(s.page(r, rolesPath+"/new"), roleForm("", nil, web.Form{})))
}

func (s Site) createRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/new") {
		return
	}
	name, permissions := posted(r)
	id, err := s.console.Roles.Create(r.Context(), actor(r), name, permissions)
	if form, status, refused := roleRefusal(err); refused {
		renderStatus(w, r, status, web.NewRole(s.page(r, rolesPath+"/new"), roleForm(name, permissions, form)))
		return
	}
	if !s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		return
	}
	toRole(w, r, id, "created")
}

// roleChanged answers a change the roles did not make for a reason that is
// not the form's — a role that is not there, a staff member no longer
// allowed, a failure — and reports whether the change was made.
func (s Site) roleChanged(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, err error) bool {
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound), errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAllowed):
		http.NotFound(w, r)
	case err != nil:
		s.failed(w, r, "a role could not be changed", err)
	default:
		return true
	}
	return false
}

// rolePage is one role. The owner role's shows what it is and offers no
// change, so it asks for no step-up; any other role's page is its forms, so
// it does.
func (s Site) rolePage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("role")
	role, err := s.console.Roles.Role(r.Context(), id)
	if errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound) {
		http.NotFound(w, r)
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "a role could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	if !role.Owner && s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/"+id) {
		return
	}
	s.showRole(w, r, http.StatusOK, role, nil, web.Form{}, roleDone[r.URL.Query().Get("done")])
}

// showRole renders role's page, with edit in place of the role's own form
// when a posted one was refused, and form's refusal otherwise.
func (s Site) showRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, role staff.Role, edit *web.RoleForm, form web.Form, done string) {
	members, err := s.console.Roles.Members(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "the staff could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	scopes, err := s.console.Roles.Scopes(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "the marketplaces could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	view := web.RolePage{Role: roleView(role), Edit: roleForm(role.Name, role.Permissions, web.Form{}), Form: form, Done: done}
	if edit != nil {
		view.Edit = *edit
	}
	for _, m := range members {
		view.Members = append(view.Members, web.Option{Value: m.ID, Label: m.Name, Detail: m.Email})
	}
	for _, scope := range scopes {
		view.Scopes = append(view.Scopes, web.Option{Value: scope.ID, Label: scope.Name})
	}
	renderStatus(w, r, status, web.Role(s.page(r, rolesPath+"/"+role.ID), view))
}

// refuseOnRole shows the role's page again with a refusal; edit is the form
// that was refused, when it was the role's own.
func (s Site) refuseOnRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, id string, status int, form web.Form, edit *web.RoleForm) {
	role, err := s.console.Roles.Role(r.Context(), id)
	if !s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		return
	}
	s.showRole(w, r, status, role, edit, form, "")
}

func (s Site) editRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("role")
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/"+id) {
		return
	}
	name, permissions := posted(r)
	err := s.console.Roles.Edit(r.Context(), actor(r), id, name, permissions)
	if form, status, refused := roleRefusal(err); refused {
		edit := roleForm(name, permissions, form)
		if errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnerRole) {
			// Not the form's fault: the owner role's page has none.
			s.refuseOnRole(w, r, id, status, form, nil)
			return
		}
		s.refuseOnRole(w, r, id, status, web.Form{}, &edit)
		return
	}
	if s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		toRole(w, r, id, "edited")
	}
}

func (s Site) assignRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("role")
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/"+id) {
		return
	}
	err := s.console.Roles.Assign(r.Context(), actor(r), id, r.PostFormValue("account"), r.PostFormValue("scope"))
	if form, status, refused := roleRefusal(err); refused {
		s.refuseOnRole(w, r, id, status, form, nil)
		return
	}
	if s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		toRole(w, r, id, "assigned")
	}
}

func (s Site) unassignRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("role")
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/"+id) {
		return
	}
	err := s.console.Roles.Unassign(r.Context(), actor(r), id, r.PostFormValue("account"), r.PostFormValue("scope"))
	if form, status, refused := roleRefusal(err); refused {
		s.refuseOnRole(w, r, id, status, form, nil)
		return
	}
	if s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		toRole(w, r, id, "unassigned")
	}
}

func (s Site) deleteRole(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	id := r.PathValue("role")
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, rolesPath+"/"+id) {
		return
	}
	err := s.console.Roles.Delete(r.Context(), actor(r), id)
	if form, status, refused := roleRefusal(err); refused {
		s.refuseOnRole(w, r, id, status, form, nil)
		return
	}
	if s.roleChanged(w, r, err) {
		http.Redirect(w, r, "/"+i18n.FromContext(r.Context())+rolesPath+"?done=deleted", http.StatusSeeOther)
	}
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add -A cmd/marketplace internal/identity internal/platform/httpx web
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
The role pages: roles listed, made, edited, assigned, removed and deleted, behind a step-up

The owner manages roles from the console (F15 spec, Flows "Roles"): a list
of the roles with what each grants and who holds it where, the owner role
first and marked as protected; a role's page with its name and permissions,
its holders each with the way to remove them, the way to assign it to a
staff member for one marketplace or the whole platform, and the way to
delete it while nobody holds it. The owner role's page shows what it is and
offers no change, and a change sent to it anyway is refused.

Every page asks for the console's view and roles.manage across the
platform, and is "not found" without them (D8). Every change asks for the
second factor again first, through F14's step-up with a new action for
changes to the platform's staff (D10), which the invitations, deactivations
and factor resets of the next pull request reuse. A refusal is said on the
field it is about, in both languages; the menu gains the roles' entry for
whoever may open them.
EOF
```


## Task 12: End-to-end: a role made, assigned, removed and deleted on the console, in both languages

The role pages in a browser, against a fresh database, as the owner uses them: the owner
role first and protected, the step-up before the first change, a refusal on the field with the choices
kept, a role assigned for one marketplace, removed and deleted, the owner role's page offering no change;
the audit log holds each change with a staff member as its actor. Without invitations the owner is the
only staff member, so the assignment is the owner's own; what a colleague sees is proved by the Go tests
until PR 3.

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/test_roles.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `e2e/console.py` (`first_run`), the `run_console` fixture (task 7); `e2e/factors.py`
  (`next_totp`); task 11's pages.
- Produces: `e2e/test_roles.py`: `test_the_owner_makes_assigns_and_deletes_a_role[pt-BR|en-US]`,
  `audited(console)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `e2e/test_roles.py`:

```python
"""The console's roles, against a fresh database (F15).

The owner, created by the first run, opens the roles: the owner role is
listed first and marked as protected. A change asks for the second factor
again first; a role is then made of declared permissions, refused on its
field when it has no name, assigned to a staff member for one marketplace,
removed from them and deleted, each audited with the owner as the actor.
The owner role's page offers no change
(docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md, D6, D7, D10).

The owner is the only staff member a pull request without invitations can
have, so the assignment here is the owner's own; a colleague's roles, and
what they then see, are proved by the Go page tests until the invitations
bring colleagues (internal/platform/httpx/roles_integration_test.go).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import psycopg
import pytest
from playwright.sync_api import expect, sync_playwright

from console import CONSOLE_HOST, OWNER_NAME, first_run
from factors import CODE_SUBMIT, next_totp, totp

# As web/locales words them.
ROLES = {"pt-BR": "Papéis", "en-US": "Roles"}
PROTECTED = {"pt-BR": "Protegido", "en-US": "Protected"}
OWNER_ROLE = {"pt-BR": "Proprietário", "en-US": "Owner"}
STAFF_STEP_UP = {
    "pt-BR": "Antes de mudar quem pode fazer o quê no console, confirme que é você.",
    "en-US": "Before changing who may do what in the console, confirm it is you.",
}
NAME_MISSING = {"pt-BR": "Dê um nome ao papel.", "en-US": "Give the role a name."}
CREATED = {"pt-BR": "O papel foi criado.", "en-US": "The role was created."}
ASSIGNED = {"pt-BR": "O papel foi atribuído.", "en-US": "The role was assigned."}
UNASSIGNED = {
    "pt-BR": "O papel foi removido do membro da equipe.",
    "en-US": "The role was removed from the staff member.",
}
DELETED = {"pt-BR": "O papel foi excluído.", "en-US": "The role was deleted."}
IN_STORE = {"pt-BR": "Em: Loja Um", "en-US": "In: Loja Um"}

# The forms of a role's page: its own, the assignment's and the deletion's.
EDIT_SUBMIT = "main form:has(input[name=name]) button[type=submit]"
ASSIGN_SUBMIT = "main form:has(select[name=account]) button[type=submit]"
REMOVE = "ul.holders form button[type=submit]"
DELETE = "main form[action$='/delete'] button[type=submit]"


def audited(console) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """The role changes the run audited, oldest first, with the kind of
    actor of each. The action and the actor's kind are the record's clear
    columns; what changed is sealed (internal/platform/audit)."""
    with psycopg.connect(console.database.owner_url) as conn:
        return conn.execute(
            "SELECT action, actor_kind FROM audit_log WHERE action LIKE 'staff.role_%' ORDER BY id"
        ).fetchall()


@pytest.mark.local_process
@pytest.mark.parametrize("language", ["pt-BR", "en-US"])
def test_the_owner_makes_assigns_and_deletes_a_role(run_console, screenshots, language):
    console = run_console()
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch()
        page = browser.new_page()
        first_run(page, console, language)
        expect(page).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/enrol$"))
        page.click("main a[href$='/account/security/app']")
        key = page.locator("#totp-key").inner_text()
        page.fill("main form input[name=label]", "Celular")
        page.fill("main form input[name=code]", totp(key))
        page.click(CODE_SUBMIT)
        expect(page.locator("ol.codes code")).to_have_count(10)

        # The roles, from the menu: the owner role first, protected.
        page.goto(console.url(CONSOLE_HOST, f"/{language}/"))
        page.click("nav.menu a[href$='/roles']")
        expect(page.locator("nav.menu a[aria-current=page]")).to_have_text(ROLES[language])
        owner_role = page.locator("ul.roles > li").first
        expect(owner_role.locator("h2 a")).to_have_text(OWNER_ROLE[language])
        expect(owner_role.locator(".protected")).to_have_text(PROTECTED[language])
        expect(owner_role.locator("ul.holders strong")).to_have_text(OWNER_NAME)
        page.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-roles-{language}.png", full_page=True)

        # A change asks for the second factor again first.
        page.click("main a[href$='/roles/new']")
        expect(page).to_have_url(re.compile(r"/account/verify\?for=staff&"))
        expect(page.locator("main")).to_contain_text(STAFF_STEP_UP[language])
        page.fill("main form input[name=code]", next_totp(key))
        page.click(CODE_SUBMIT)
        expect(page).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/roles/new$"))
        expect(page.locator("input[name=permission]")).to_have_count(6)
        page.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-role-new-{language}.png", full_page=True)

        # A role with no name is refused on its field, its choices kept.
        page.fill("main form input[name=name]", " ")
        page.check("input[name=permission][value='console.view']")
        page.click(EDIT_SUBMIT)
        expect(page.locator("[role=alert]")).to_have_text(NAME_MISSING[language])
        expect(page.locator("main form input[name=name]")).to_be_focused()
        expect(page.locator("input[name=permission][value='console.view']")).to_be_checked()
        page.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-role-refused-{language}.png", full_page=True)

        page.fill("main form input[name=name]", "Suporte")
        page.check("input[name=permission][value='staff.invite']")
        page.click(EDIT_SUBMIT)
        expect(page).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/roles/[0-9a-f-]{{36}}\?done=created$"))
        expect(page.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(CREATED[language])

        # Assigned for one marketplace, removed, and deleted.
        page.select_option("select[name=scope]", label="Loja Um")
        page.click(ASSIGN_SUBMIT)
        expect(page.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(ASSIGNED[language])
        expect(page.locator("ul.holders li")).to_contain_text(IN_STORE[language])
        expect(page.locator(DELETE)).to_have_count(0)
        page.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-role-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        page.click(REMOVE)
        expect(page.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(UNASSIGNED[language])
        page.click(DELETE)
        expect(page).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/roles\?done=deleted$"))
        expect(page.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(DELETED[language])
        expect(page.locator("ul.roles > li")).to_have_count(1)

        # The owner role's page offers no change.
        page.click("ul.roles h2 a")
        expect(page.locator("main h1 .protected")).to_have_text(PROTECTED[language])
        expect(page.locator("main form")).to_have_count(0)
        page.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-owner-role-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        browser.close()

    assert audited(console) == [
        ("staff.role_created", "staff"),
        ("staff.role_assigned", "staff"),
        ("staff.role_unassigned", "staff"),
        ("staff.role_deleted", "staff"),
    ]
```

# PR 3 — Invitations, deactivation and factor reset

Branch `claude/funny-wright-379asb-f15staff` from `main` once PR 2 is merged. A permission's reach, the factor reset with its mailed link, deactivation, the staff list, and invitations, with their mails and step-ups.

## Task 13: A permission's reach: the platform's staff and roles are managed across the platform only

D6's reach (the owner's ruling on PR 2's open points, already in the spec): each permission
declares where a role granting it may be assigned. `console.view` and `console.version` reach any scope;
the four permissions over the platform's staff and roles reach the whole platform only. The service
refuses assigning such a role for one marketplace and giving such a permission to a role somebody holds
for one marketplace; the role's page says why, offers "the whole platform" alone where it is the only
place, and marks the platform-only permissions. The migrator writes each permission's reach with its key
(`permission.platform_only`), and two triggers refuse the same rows to SQL that goes around the service.

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00020_permission_reach.sql`
- Modify: `internal/staff/permissions.go`, `internal/staff/permissions_test.go`, `internal/staff/roles.go`, `internal/staff/roles_integration_test.go`, `web/roles.go`, `web/layout.templ`, `web/roles.templ`, `web/locales/en-US.json`, `web/locales/pt-BR.json`, `internal/platform/httpx/roles.go`, `internal/platform/httpx/roles_integration_test.go`, `cmd/marketplace/migrate_integration_test.go`, `e2e/test_roles.py`
- Regenerated by `make generate` (committed): the `*_templ.go` of every template this task touches

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: the registry, `Synchronise` (task 8); `Roles.Assign`, `Roles.Edit`, `lockRole`,
  `checkPermissions`, `violates` (task 9); `roleRefusal`, `roleView`, `roleForm` (task 11).
- Produces: `type Reach int`, `Anywhere`, `PlatformOnly`; `func (p Permission) Reach() Reach`;
  `staff.ErrPlatformOnly`, `staff.ErrHeldInAMarketplace`; `func (r Role) PlatformOnly() bool`; private
  `platformOnly([]Permission) bool`, `notHeldInAMarketplace(ctx, tx, role)`; migration
  `00020_permission_reach.sql` (`permission.platform_only`, `user_role_within_reach()`,
  `role_permission_within_reach()`, constraint name `role_within_reach`); `web.RoleView.PlatformOnly`,
  `web.Choice.PlatformOnly`, `web.ScopeHint(role RoleView) []string`; locale keys
  `console.roles.platform_only`, `console.roles.platform_only_scope`, `console.roles.error.platform_only`,
  `console.roles.error.held_in_a_marketplace`.

with:

```go
}

// A role granting a permission of the whole platform only is assigned
// across the platform and nowhere else, and a role somebody holds for one
// marketplace is given no such permission: the service refuses both, the
// database refuses them again to SQL of its own, and a role within its
// reach is assigned for one marketplace as before (the owner's ruling on
// F15 PR 2).
func TestAPlatformOnlyPermissionIsHeldAcrossThePlatformOnly(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	managers, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Managers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.RolesManage})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), managers, tm.colleague, tm.one); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrPlatformOnly) {
		t.Fatalf("a platform-only role assigned for one marketplace = %v, want ErrPlatformOnly", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), managers, tm.colleague, staff.Platform); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a platform-only role assigned across the platform = %v", err)
	}

	viewers, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a role within its reach assigned for one marketplace = %v", err)
	}
	for _, p := range []staff.Permission{staff.StaffInvite, staff.StaffDeactivate, staff.StaffResetFactor, staff.RolesManage} {
		if err := tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, p}); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrHeldInAMarketplace) {
			t.Errorf("giving %s to a role held for one marketplace = %v, want ErrHeldInAMarketplace", p, err)
		}
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.ConsoleVersion}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("giving a permission within its reach = %v", err)
	}
	role, err := tm.roles.Role(t.Context(), viewers)
	if err != nil || role.PlatformOnly() || !slices.Equal(role.Permissions, []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.ConsoleVersion}) {
		t.Fatalf("the role held for one marketplace is %+v, %v", role, err)
	}

	// The database, to SQL that goes around the service.
	for _, refused := range []struct {
		statement string
		args      []any
	}{
		{`INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id, marketplace_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`, []any{tm.colleague, managers, tm.two}},
		{`UPDATE user_role SET marketplace_id = $1 WHERE role_id = $2`, []any{tm.two, managers}},
		{`INSERT INTO role_permission (role_id, permission_key) VALUES ($1, 'staff.invite')`, []any{viewers}},
	} {
		wantRefused(t, tm.database, "23514", refused.statement, refused.args...)
	}
	var reach []string
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		rows, err := tx.Query(t.Context(), `SELECT key FROM permission WHERE platform_only ORDER BY key`)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		reach, err = pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowTo[string])
		return err
	}); err != nil || !slices.Equal(reach, []string{"roles.manage", "staff.deactivate", "staff.invite", "staff.reset_factor"}) {
		t.Fatalf("the database holds %v as platform-only (%v)", reach, err)
	}
}

	tm := newTeam(t)
```

```go
// A role granting a permission of the whole platform only offers the whole
// platform alone as where it applies, says why on its page, and is refused
// for one marketplace on the field that chose it; a role somebody holds for
// one marketplace is refused such a permission on its own form (the owner's
// ruling on F15 PR 2).
func TestAPlatformOnlyRoleIsAssignedAcrossThePlatformOnly(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	owner, key := c.signedInOwner(t)
	_, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "colega@example.test")
	managers, err := c.roles.Create(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), "Gestão", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.RolesManage})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	stepUpOnConsole(t, owner, key, "/roles/"+managers)
	page := owner.get("/roles/" + managers)
	body := page.Body.String()
	if page.Code != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(body, `<option value="`+c.marketplace.ID+`"`) ||
		!strings.Contains(body, "Este papel dá uma permissão que só vale para toda a plataforma") ||
		!strings.Contains(body, `<small class="reach">Só para toda a plataforma</small>`) {
		t.Fatalf("the platform-only role's page: status %d, body %s", page.Code, body)
	}
	refused := owner.post("/roles/"+managers+"/assign", url.Values{"account": {account}, "scope": {c.marketplace.ID}})
	wantStatus(t, refused, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Este papel dá uma permissão que só vale para toda a plataforma: atribua-o")
	if !strings.Contains(refused.Body.String(), `name="scope" aria-describedby="form-error scope-hint" aria-invalid="true"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refusal is not on the scope: %s", refused.Body.String())
	}
	if _, ok := c.trail.find("staff.role_assigned"); ok {
		t.Fatal("a refused assignment was audited")
	}

	viewers := c.grant(t, account, c.marketplace.ID, staff.ConsoleView)
	held := owner.post("/roles/"+viewers, url.Values{"name": {"Leitura"}, "permission": {"console.view", "staff.invite"}})
	wantStatus(t, held, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Alguém tem este papel em um marketplace")
	if !strings.Contains(held.Body.String(), `value="staff.invite" checked`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refused form lost what was ticked: %s", held.Body.String())
	}
}
```

Create `migrations/00020_permission_reach.sql`:

```sql
-- Where a role granting a permission may be assigned
-- (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md, D6).
--
-- Some permissions are over what belongs to no marketplace — the platform's
-- staff and its roles: inviting, deactivating, resetting a factor, managing
-- roles. A role that grants one of them is assigned across the platform or
-- not at all; held for one marketplace it would mean nothing the console
-- could honour. The registry in internal/staff declares each permission's
-- reach, and the migration job writes it here with the permission
-- (staff.Synchronise), so this column is the code's word like the key.
--
-- The service refuses such an assignment, and the refusal is said on the
-- role's page; the triggers below refuse it again, to the application role
-- writing SQL of its own:
--
-- * an assignment for one marketplace of a role that grants a platform-only
--   permission (user_role_within_reach);
-- * a platform-only permission given to a role somebody holds for one
--   marketplace (role_permission_within_reach).
--
-- Both are check violations named `role_within_reach`, which is how the
-- service recognises them. A deploy that narrowed a declared permission's
-- reach would leave the assignments made before it alone: the triggers look
-- at the rows written, not at the rows already there. No permission of F15
-- changes its reach; one that does is a change of its own, with its own
-- migration.

-- +goose Up
ALTER TABLE permission ADD COLUMN platform_only boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false;

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION user_role_within_reach() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF NEW.marketplace_id IS NOT NULL AND EXISTS (
        SELECT 1 FROM role_permission rp JOIN permission p ON p.key = rp.permission_key
         WHERE rp.role_id = NEW.role_id AND p.platform_only) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'a role granting a permission of the whole platform is assigned for the whole platform only'
            USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', CONSTRAINT = 'role_within_reach';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION role_permission_within_reach() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM permission WHERE key = NEW.permission_key AND platform_only)
       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = NEW.role_id AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'a role held for one marketplace is given no permission of the whole platform'
            USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', CONSTRAINT = 'role_within_reach';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

CREATE TRIGGER user_role_within_reach
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF role_id, marketplace_id ON user_role
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION user_role_within_reach();
CREATE TRIGGER role_permission_within_reach
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE ON role_permission
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION role_permission_within_reach();

-- +goose Down
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS role_permission_within_reach ON role_permission;
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS user_role_within_reach ON user_role;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS role_permission_within_reach();
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS user_role_within_reach();
ALTER TABLE permission DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS platform_only;
```

```go
}

// Known reports whether p is a declared permission.
func Known(p Permission) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(registry, func(d declared) bool { return d.permission == p })
}

// Reach is where a role granting p may be assigned; a permission nobody
// declared reaches nowhere a role can hold it, and is refused before this is
// asked.
func (p Permission) Reach() Reach {
	for _, d := range registry {
		if d.permission == p {
			return d.reach
		}
	}
	return Anywhere
}
```

In `internal/staff/permissions.go`, replace:

```go

// Synchronise makes the permission table hold exactly the declared
// permissions, and reports what it added and what it removed. A permission
// the code no longer declares goes, and with it every role's hold on it
// (role_permission cascades). The migration job runs it on every deploy,
// after the schema, as the role that owns the tables: the service may only
// read them (internal/platform/db).
func Synchronise(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx) (added, removed []Permission, err error) {
	keys := make([]string, len(registry))
	for i, p := range registry {
		keys[i] = string(p)
	}
	added, err = collect(ctx, tx, `
		INSERT INTO permission (key) SELECT unnest($1::text[])
		ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING RETURNING key`, keys)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
```

with:

```go

// Synchronise makes the permission table hold exactly the declared
// permissions, each with its reach, and reports what it added and what it
// removed. A permission the code no longer declares goes, and with it every
// role's hold on it (role_permission cascades). The migration job runs it on
// every deploy, after the schema, as the role that owns the tables: the
// service may only read them (internal/platform/db). What it removes is
// logged by the job, not audited: no person removed it, a deploy did, and
// the code change is its record (F15 spec, Audit).
func Synchronise(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx) (added, removed []Permission, err error) {
	keys := make([]string, len(registry))
	platformOnly := make([]bool, len(registry))
	for i, d := range registry {
		keys[i], platformOnly[i] = string(d.permission), d.reach == PlatformOnly
	}
	added, err = collect(ctx, tx, `
		INSERT INTO permission (key, platform_only) SELECT * FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::boolean[])
		ON CONFLICT (key) DO NOTHING RETURNING key`, keys, platformOnly)
	if err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
	if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
		UPDATE permission p SET platform_only = d.platform_only
		  FROM unnest($1::text[], $2::boolean[]) AS d (key, platform_only)
		 WHERE p.key = d.key AND p.platform_only <> d.platform_only`, keys, platformOnly); err != nil {
		return nil, nil, err
	}
```

```go
// PlatformOnly reports whether the role grants a permission of the whole
// platform only, and is therefore assigned across the platform or not at
// all. The owner role is: it grants every permission, and is the platform's.
func (r Role) PlatformOnly() bool {
	return r.Owner || platformOnly(r.Permissions)
}

// platformOnly reports whether any of permissions reaches the whole platform
// only.
func platformOnly(permissions []Permission) bool {
	return slices.ContainsFunc(permissions, func(p Permission) bool { return p.Reach() == PlatformOnly })
}
```

```go
// notHeldInAMarketplace refuses, with ErrHeldInAMarketplace, a role
// somebody holds for one marketplace: it cannot be given a permission of the
// whole platform only.
func notHeldInAMarketplace(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, role string) error {
	var held bool
	if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = $1 AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL)`, role).Scan(&held); err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if held {
		return ErrHeldInAMarketplace
	}
	return nil
}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add -A cmd/marketplace e2e internal/platform/httpx internal/staff migrations web
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
A permission's reach: the platform's staff and roles are managed across the platform only

The owner ruled on PR 2's open points: each permission in the registry
declares where a role granting it may be assigned. The console's own home
and build reach every scope, so a staff member of one marketplace signs in
to the same console; inviting, deactivating, resetting a factor and
managing roles are about what belongs to no marketplace, so a role granting
one of them is held across the platform or not at all. Only a staff member
holding staff.invite across the platform will be able to invite.

The service refuses an assignment for one marketplace of such a role, and
refuses to give such a permission to a role somebody holds for one
marketplace; the role's page says why, in both languages, offers the whole
platform alone where it is the only place, and marks the platform-only
permissions on the form. The migrator writes each permission's reach with
its key, and two triggers refuse the same rows to SQL that goes around the
service, as the owner role's are refused.
EOF
```


## Task 14: A colleague's second factor reset and a staff member deactivated, the owner kept by the database

D9 at the service. A reset takes every app, key and recovery code of a colleague, ends every
session and every sign-in waiting for its second step, and tells them by e-mail who did it (task 15 then
makes the notice carry the link that alone brings a factor back). A deactivation marks the account, ends
its sessions and removes its roles; a sign-in with it is answered as a wrong password is, after the same
argon2 work, and recorded with the reason. The identity service changes the account's own rows, in the
order every other path takes them, and runs the console's check and record in the same transaction
(`StaffChange`): only a staff member allowed across the platform changes a colleague, nobody changes their
own account this way, and nobody deactivates the owner or resets the owner's factor — which a trigger on
`account` refuses again. The staff list's reading is here too.

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00021_staff_offboarding.sql`, `internal/staff/team.go`, `internal/staff/team_integration_test.go`, `web/mail/second-factor-reset.en-US.html`, `web/mail/second-factor-reset.en-US.txt`, `web/mail/second-factor-reset.pt-BR.html`, `web/mail/second-factor-reset.pt-BR.txt`, `web/mail/staff-deactivated.en-US.html`, `web/mail/staff-deactivated.en-US.txt`, `web/mail/staff-deactivated.pt-BR.html`, `web/mail/staff-deactivated.pt-BR.txt`
- Modify: `internal/identity/identity.go`, `internal/identity/staff.go`, `internal/identity/staff_integration_test.go`, `internal/identity/store.go`, `internal/staff/roles.go`, `internal/staff/roles_integration_test.go`, `internal/platform/mail/templates_test.go`, `docs/infrastructure.md`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `identity.lockAccount`, `revokeSessions`, `notify`, `clearFailures`, `Visit.Platform`;
  `staff.Roles`, `allows`, `isOwner`-style reads of `user_role.owner` (task 9); the mail templates' flat
  variables and `sampleVariables`.
- Produces: migration `00021_staff_offboarding.sql` (`account.deactivated_at`, `account.factor_reset_at`,
  `account_only_staff_are_offboarded`, trigger `account_keeps_the_owner`); `identity.Account.DeactivatedAt`;
  `identity.ErrStaffUnknown`, `identity.ErrDeactivated`;
  `type StaffChange struct{ Check, Then func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, target Account) error; By string }`;
  `func (s *Service) ResetFactors(ctx context.Context, v Visit, target string, change StaffChange) error`;
  `func (s *Service) Deactivate(ctx context.Context, v Visit, target string, change StaffChange) error`;
  `revokeSessions(ctx, tx, account, reason string, now time.Time)`; `staff.Actor.Name`;
  `staff.ErrOwnAccount`, `staff.ErrOwnerProtected`; `type Colleague`, `type Held`, `type Team`;
  `func NewTeam(db Transactor, service *identity.Service, auditor Auditor) *Team`;
  `(t *Team) List(ctx) ([]Colleague, error)`, `Colleague(ctx, id string) (Colleague, error)`,
  `ResetFactor(ctx, actor Actor, v identity.Visit, account string) error` (audits `staff.factor_reset`),
  `Deactivate(ctx, actor Actor, v identity.Visit, account string) error` (audits `staff.deactivated`);
  mail `second-factor-reset` and `staff-deactivated` (`{{.Name}}`, `{{.By}}`, `{{.From}}`).

```go
// noticeTo is the variables of the last mail of template requested for an
// address on the platform, or fails the test when there is none.
func noticeTo(t *testing.T, db serving, template, to string) map[string]string {
	t.Helper()
	variables := map[string]string{}
	if err := db.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `
			SELECT payload->'Variables' FROM outbox_event
			 WHERE kind = 'email.send' AND payload->>'Template' = $1 AND payload->>'To' = $2
			 ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`, template, to).Scan(&variables)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("no %s mail to %s was requested: %v", template, to, err)
	}
	return variables
}

// revoked counts an account's sessions ended for reason.
func revoked(t *testing.T, db serving, account, reason string) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	if err := db.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(),
			`SELECT count(*) FROM session WHERE account_id = $1 AND revoked_reason = $2`, account, reason).Scan(&n)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return n
}

// A colleague's reset of a staff member's second factor takes every method
// and recovery code, ends every session and every sign-in waiting for its
// second step, and tells the owner of the account who did it; the next
// sign-in, with the password alone, opens a session with no second factor,
// which the console sends to enrol a new one (F15 spec, D9).
func TestAResetEndsEverySessionAndAsksForANewFactor(t *testing.T) {
	s, db, _, _, _ := service(t)
	sealed(t, s)
	email, token := staffMember(t, s)
	session, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	enrolApp(t, s, platformVisit(), session, "phone")
	waiting, err := s.SignIn(t.Context(), platformVisit(), email, "correct horse battery staple")
	if !errors.Is(err, ErrSecondStep) {
		t.Fatalf("SignIn with an app = %v, want ErrSecondStep", err)
	}

	var checked, then string
	if err := s.ResetFactors(t.Context(), platformVisit(), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{
		By:    "Dona",
		Check: func(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, target Account) error { checked = target.Email; return nil },
		Then:  func(_ context.Context, _ pgx.Tx, target Account) error { then = target.ID; return nil },
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ResetFactors = %v", err)
	}
	if checked != email || then != session.Account.ID {
		t.Fatalf("the change's check saw %q and its record %q", checked, then)
	}
	if _, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token); !errors.Is(err, ErrSessionInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("the session before the reset = %v, want ErrSessionInvalid", err)
	}
	if _, err := s.Pending(t.Context(), platformVisit(), waiting, ""); !errors.Is(err, ErrChallengeInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("the sign-in waiting for its second step = %v, want ErrChallengeInvalid", err)
	}
	if n := revoked(t, db, session.Account.ID, "factor_reset"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d sessions were ended for the reset, want 1", n)
	}

	again, err := s.SignIn(t.Context(), platformVisit(), email, "correct horse battery staple")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("SignIn after the reset = %v, want a session with no second step", err)
	}
	fresh, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), again)
	if err != nil || fresh.SecondFactor {
		t.Fatalf("the session after the reset is %+v, %v; want one with no second factor", fresh, err)
	}
	security, err := s.Security(t.Context(), platformVisit(), fresh)
	if err != nil || len(security.Factors) != 0 || security.RecoveryIssued != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("after the reset the account has %+v, %v; want nothing", security, err)
	}
	if notice := noticeTo(t, db, "second-factor-reset", email); notice["By"] != "Dona" || notice["Name"] != "Staff Member" {
		t.Fatalf("the reset's notice carries %v", notice)
	}
}

// A deactivated staff member signs in no more: the right password is
// answered as a wrong one is, and recorded as refused with the reason; every
// session ends; the owner of the account is told; and nothing more is done
// to the account once it is deactivated (F15 spec, D9).
func TestADeactivatedStaffMemberIsAnsweredAsAWrongPasswordIs(t *testing.T) {
	s, db, _, _, trail := service(t)
	email, token := staffMember(t, s)
	session, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := s.Deactivate(t.Context(), platformVisit(), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{By: "Dona"}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Deactivate = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token); !errors.Is(err, ErrSessionInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("the session of a deactivated account = %v, want ErrSessionInvalid", err)
	}
	if n := revoked(t, db, session.Account.ID, "deactivated"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d sessions were ended for the deactivation, want 1", n)
	}
	for _, password := range []string{"correct horse battery staple", "not the password at all"} {
		if _, err := s.SignIn(t.Context(), platformVisit(), email, password); !errors.Is(err, ErrCredentials) {
			t.Fatalf("SignIn of a deactivated account = %v, want ErrCredentials", err)
		}
	}
	reasons := 0
	for _, e := range trail.entries {
		if e.Action == "identity.signin_failed" && e.Subject.ID == session.Account.ID && strings.Contains(string(e.After), `"reason":"deactivated"`) {
			reasons++
		}
	}
	if reasons != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d refusals recorded the deactivation as their reason, want the right password's one", reasons)
	}
	if notice := noticeTo(t, db, "staff-deactivated", email); notice["By"] != "Dona" {
		t.Fatalf("the deactivation's notice carries %v", notice)
	}
	for what, err := range map[string]error{
		"deactivated again": s.Deactivate(t.Context(), platformVisit(), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{}),
		"reset":             s.ResetFactors(t.Context(), platformVisit(), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{}),
	} {
		if !errors.Is(err, ErrDeactivated) {
			t.Errorf("a deactivated account %s = %v, want ErrDeactivated", what, err)
		}
	}
}

// A change to a staff account changes nothing when its check refuses it, and
// reaches no account that is not a staff member's.
func TestAChangeToAStaffAccountIsRefusedWhole(t *testing.T) {
	s, db, one, _, _ := service(t)
	_, token := staffMember(t, s)
	session, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	refused := errors.New("refused")
	for _, change := range []func(context.Context, Visit, string, StaffChange) error{s.ResetFactors, s.Deactivate} {
		err := change(t.Context(), platformVisit(), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{
			Check: func(context.Context, pgx.Tx, Account) error { return refused },
		})
		if !errors.Is(err, refused) {
			t.Fatalf("a change its check refuses = %v, want the check's error", err)
		}
	}
	if _, err := s.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("a refused change ended the session: %v", err)
	}

	confirmed(t, s, db, one, "buyer-"+unique(t, "x")+"@example.test")
	var buyer string
	if err := db.InTxFor(t.Context(), one, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT id::text FROM account ORDER BY created_at DESC LIMIT 1`).Scan(&buyer)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, target := range []string{buyer, "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009", "not-an-id"} {
		if err := s.Deactivate(t.Context(), platformVisit(), target, StaffChange{}); !errors.Is(err, ErrStaffUnknown) {
			t.Errorf("deactivating %q = %v, want ErrStaffUnknown", target, err)
		}
	}
	if err := s.ResetFactors(t.Context(), visit(one), session.Account.ID, StaffChange{}); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a reset on a marketplace's visit was made")
	}
}
```

Create `internal/staff/team_integration_test.go`:

```go
//go:build integration

package staff_test

import (
	"errors"
	"slices"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
)

// member creates a staff member who holds a role granting permissions
// across the platform, and returns their account.
func (tm team) member(t *testing.T, name, email string, permissions ...staff.Permission) string {
	t.Helper()
	account := tm.newStaff(t, name, email)
	role, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), name+" role", permissions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), role, account, staff.Platform); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return account
}

// withFactor gives account an app and a recovery code, written as the
// migrator writes, since the flows that add them are F14's and tested there.
func (tm team) withFactor(t *testing.T, account string) {
	t.Helper()
	for _, statement := range []string{
		`INSERT INTO second_factor (account_id, kind, label, secret, totp_last_step) VALUES ($1, 'totp', 'phone', '\x00', 0)`,
		`INSERT INTO recovery_code (account_id, code_hash) VALUES ($1, '\x01')`,
	} {
		if _, err := tm.database.pool.Exec(t.Context(), statement, account); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
}

// count counts the rows a statement about account finds, as the application
// on the platform.
func (tm team) count(t *testing.T, statement, account string) int {
	t.Helper()
	var n int
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), statement, account).Scan(&n)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return n
}

// signIn signs account's address in on the platform, and returns the
// session's token and the error.
func (tm team) signIn(t *testing.T, email string) (string, error) {
	t.Helper()
	return tm.service.SignIn(t.Context(), platform(), email, password)
}

// Only a staff member who may reset factors across the platform resets a
// colleague's: every method, recovery code and session of the colleague
// goes, the next sign-in asks for no second step, and the reset is audited
// with the staff member as its actor and what was removed. Nobody resets
// their own, and nobody the owner's (D9).
func TestOnlyWhoMayResetAFactorResetsAColleagues(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	resetter := tm.member(t, "Resetter", "resetter@example.test", staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffResetFactor)
	tm.withFactor(t, tm.colleague)
	token, err := tm.signIn(t, "colleague@example.test")
	if !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrSecondStep) {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague signs in with a factor = %v, want ErrSecondStep", err)
	}

	for _, refused := range []struct {
		actor, target string
		want          error
	}{
		{tm.colleague, tm.colleague, staff.ErrNotAllowed},
		{resetter, resetter, staff.ErrOwnAccount},
		{resetter, tm.owner, staff.ErrOwnerProtected},
		{tm.owner, tm.owner, staff.ErrOwnAccount},
		{"not-an-id", tm.colleague, staff.ErrNotAllowed},
	} {
		if err := tm.team.ResetFactor(t.Context(), by(refused.actor), platform(), refused.target); !errors.Is(err, refused.want) {
			t.Errorf("a reset by %s of %s = %v, want %v", refused.actor, refused.target, err, refused.want)
		}
	}
	if err := tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), by(resetter), platform(), tm.colleague); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAllowed) {
		t.Fatalf("a deactivation by who may only reset = %v, want ErrNotAllowed", err)
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM second_factor WHERE account_id = $1`, tm.colleague); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the refused resets left %d factors, want 1", n)
	}

	if err := tm.team.ResetFactor(t.Context(), by(resetter), platform(), tm.colleague); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("ResetFactor = %v", err)
	}
	for statement, want := range map[string]int{
		`SELECT count(*) FROM second_factor WHERE account_id = $1`:                               0,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM recovery_code WHERE account_id = $1`:                               0,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM sign_in_challenge WHERE account_id = $1`:                           0,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM session WHERE account_id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL`:              0,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM account WHERE id = $1 AND factor_reset_at IS NOT NULL`:             1,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM user_role WHERE account_id = $1`:                                   0,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM account WHERE id = $1 AND deactivated_at IS NULL`:                  1,
		`SELECT count(*) FROM session WHERE account_id = $1 AND revoked_reason = 'factor_reset'`: 1,
	} {
		if n := tm.count(t, statement, tm.colleague); n != want {
			t.Errorf("after the reset %s = %d, want %d", statement, n, want)
		}
	}
	if _, err := tm.service.Pending(t.Context(), platform(), token, ""); !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrChallengeInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("the sign-in waiting for its second step = %v, want ErrChallengeInvalid", err)
	}
	again, err := tm.signIn(t, "colleague@example.test")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague signs in after the reset = %v, want a session", err)
	}
	if session, err := tm.service.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), again); err != nil || session.SecondFactor {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague's session after the reset is %+v, %v; want one with no factor", session, err)
	}

	reset, found := tm.trail.find("staff.factor_reset")
	if !found || reset.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: resetter, Kind: audit.Staff}) ||
		reset.Subject != (audit.Subject{Kind: "account", ID: tm.colleague, Person: tm.colleague}) || reset.From != "203.0.113.5" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.factor_reset = %+v (found %v)", reset, found)
	}
	if factors, _ := decoded(t, reset.Before)["factors"].([]any); len(factors) != 1 || factors[0] != "totp" {
		t.Fatalf("staff.factor_reset says %s was removed", reset.Before)
	}
}

// decoded is a state an audit entry carries.
func decoded(t *testing.T, raw []byte) map[string]any {
	t.Helper()
	return state(t, audit.Entry{After: raw})
}

// A deactivated staff member signs in no more, their sessions end and their
// roles go; the staff list shows them apart, the role pages offer them no
// more, and the deactivation is audited with the roles they held (D9).
func TestADeactivatedStaffMemberLosesTheirRolesAndSessions(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	viewers, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	token, err := tm.signIn(t, "colleague@example.test")
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}

	if err := tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), platform(), tm.colleague); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Deactivate = %v", err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.service.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), token); !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrSessionInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("the deactivated member's session = %v, want ErrSessionInvalid", err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.signIn(t, "colleague@example.test"); !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrCredentials) {
		t.Fatalf("the deactivated member signing in = %v, want ErrCredentials", err)
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.colleague, staff.ConsoleView, tm.one, false)
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM user_role WHERE account_id = $1`, tm.colleague); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the deactivated member holds %d roles, want none", n)
	}

	deactivated, found := tm.trail.find("staff.deactivated")
	if !found || deactivated.Actor.ID != tm.owner || deactivated.Subject.Person != tm.colleague {
		t.Fatalf("staff.deactivated = %+v (found %v)", deactivated, found)
	}
	if roles, _ := decoded(t, deactivated.Before)["roles"].([]any); len(roles) != 1 ||
		roles[0].(map[string]any)["role"] != viewers || roles[0].(map[string]any)["scope"] != tm.one {
		t.Fatalf("staff.deactivated says the roles were %s", deactivated.Before)
	}

	colleagues, err := tm.team.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil || len(colleagues) != 2 || colleagues[0].ID != tm.owner || colleagues[1].ID != tm.colleague ||
		colleagues[1].DeactivatedAt == nil || len(colleagues[1].Roles) != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the staff list is %+v, %v; want the owner, then the deactivated member", colleagues, err)
	}
	members, err := tm.roles.Members(t.Context())
	if err != nil || len(members) != 1 || members[0].ID != tm.owner {
		t.Fatalf("the members a role can be assigned to are %+v, %v; want the owner alone", members, err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, tm.colleague, staff.Platform); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotStaff) {
		t.Fatalf("a role assigned to a deactivated member = %v, want ErrNotStaff", err)
	}
	for what, err := range map[string]error{
		"deactivated again": tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), platform(), tm.colleague),
		"reset":             tm.team.ResetFactor(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), platform(), tm.colleague),
	} {
		if !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrDeactivated) {
			t.Errorf("a deactivated member %s = %v, want ErrDeactivated", what, err)
		}
	}
}

// The owner is never deactivated, and their second factor is reset by
// nobody: the service refuses whoever tries, the owner included, and the
// database refuses the same rows to SQL of its own (D7, D9).
func TestTheOwnerIsNeitherDeactivatedNorReset(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	manager := tm.member(t, "Manager", "manager@example.test", staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffDeactivate, staff.StaffResetFactor)
	for what, err := range map[string]error{
		"deactivated by a manager": tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), by(manager), platform(), tm.owner),
		"reset by a manager":       tm.team.ResetFactor(t.Context(), by(manager), platform(), tm.owner),
	} {
		if !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnerProtected) {
			t.Errorf("the owner %s = %v, want ErrOwnerProtected", what, err)
		}
	}
	if err := tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), platform(), tm.owner); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnAccount) {
		t.Fatalf("the owner deactivating themselves = %v, want ErrOwnAccount", err)
	}

	var buyer string
	if err := tm.database.pool.QueryRow(t.Context(), `
		INSERT INTO account (marketplace_id, kind, email, email_normalised, name)
		VALUES ($1, 'buyer', 'buyer@example.test', 'buyer@example.test', 'Buyer') RETURNING id::text`, tm.one).Scan(&buyer); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, refused := range []struct {
		code, statement string
		args            []any
	}{
		{"23001", `UPDATE account SET deactivated_at = now() WHERE id = $1`, []any{tm.owner}},
		{"23001", `UPDATE account SET factor_reset_at = now() WHERE id = $1`, []any{tm.owner}},
	} {
		wantRefused(t, tm.database, refused.code, refused.statement, refused.args...)
	}
	if _, err := tm.database.pool.Exec(t.Context(), `UPDATE account SET deactivated_at = now() WHERE id = $1`, buyer); err == nil {
		t.Fatal("a buyer's account was deactivated")
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM account WHERE id = $1 AND deactivated_at IS NULL AND factor_reset_at IS NULL`, tm.owner); n != 1 {
		t.Fatal("the owner was deactivated or reset")
	}
	if _, err := tm.signIn(t, "owner@example.test"); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("the owner signs in = %v", err)
	}
	tm.allows(t, tm.owner, staff.RolesManage, staff.Platform, true)
}

// The staff list shows every member with the roles they hold where, the
// kinds of second factor they have, and who is the owner.
func TestTheStaffListShowsRolesAndSecondFactors(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	viewers, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, tm.colleague, tm.one); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	tm.withFactor(t, tm.colleague)

	colleagues, err := tm.team.List(t.Context())
	if err != nil || len(colleagues) != 2 {
		t.Fatalf("List = %+v, %v", colleagues, err)
	}
	colleague, owner := colleagues[0], colleagues[1]
	if colleague.ID != tm.colleague || colleague.Owner || !slices.Equal(colleague.Factors, []identity.Method{identity.MethodApp}) ||
		len(colleague.Roles) != 1 || colleague.Roles[0] != (staff.Held{Role: viewers, Name: "Viewers", Marketplace: tm.one, MarketplaceName: "One"}) {
		t.Fatalf("the colleague is listed as %+v", colleague)
	}
	if owner.ID != tm.owner || !owner.Owner || len(owner.Factors) != 0 || len(owner.Roles) != 1 || !owner.Roles[0].Owner ||
		owner.Roles[0].Marketplace != staff.Platform {
		t.Fatalf("the owner is listed as %+v", owner)
	}
	one, err := tm.team.Colleague(t.Context(), tm.colleague)
	if err != nil || one.ID != tm.colleague || len(one.Roles) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("Colleague = %+v, %v", one, err)
	}
	for _, missing := range []string{"00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009", "not-an-id"} {
		if _, err := tm.team.Colleague(t.Context(), missing); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotStaff) {
			t.Errorf("Colleague(%q) = %v, want ErrNotStaff", missing, err)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
// The notices of a colleague's change to a staff account exist in both
// languages and both parts, name the reader, the colleague who made the
// change and the console, and say what the reader does next (F15 spec, D9).
func TestTheStaffNoticesNameWhoMadeTheChange(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct{ template, language, want string }{
		{"second-factor-reset", "en-US", "add a new app or key"},
		{"second-factor-reset", "pt-BR", "adicionar um novo aplicativo ou chave"},
		{"staff-deactivated", "en-US", "you can no longer sign in"},
		{"staff-deactivated", "pt-BR", "você não pode mais entrar"},
	} {
		rendered, err := templates(t).Render(mail.Message{
			Template: tc.template, Language: tc.language, To: "reader@example.test", From: "Console",
			Variables: map[string]string{"Name": "Leitora", "By": "Dona"},
		}, i18n.Default)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("%s in %s: %v", tc.template, tc.language, err)
		}
		if rendered.Language != tc.language || !strings.Contains(rendered.Subject, "Console") {
			t.Errorf("%s in %s: language %s, subject %q", tc.template, tc.language, rendered.Language, rendered.Subject)
		}
		for _, part := range []string{rendered.Text, rendered.HTML} {
			for _, want := range []string{"Leitora", "Dona", "Console", tc.want} {
				if !strings.Contains(part, want) {
					t.Errorf("%s in %s does not say %q: %s", tc.template, tc.language, want, part)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
```

Create `migrations/00021_staff_offboarding.sql`:

```sql
-- A staff member deactivated, and a colleague's second factor reset
-- (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md, D9).
--
-- `deactivated_at` is when a staff account was deactivated: it signs in no
-- more, and is answered as a wrong password is. Reactivation is not in F15,
-- so nothing clears it. `factor_reset_at` is when a colleague last reset the
-- account's second factor: every method, recovery code and session went, and
-- the console asks for a new app or key at the next sign-in, as it does of a
-- staff member who never had one. Both are staff's alone: a buyer is neither
-- deactivated nor reset by a colleague.
--
-- The owner cannot be deactivated, and nobody but the owner changes the
-- owner's second factors — which, since nobody resets their own this way,
-- means a reset never reaches the owner. The service refuses both; the
-- trigger below refuses them again to the application role writing SQL of
-- its own, and to a person with the migrator's access, as the owner role's
-- triggers do (migrations/00019_roles.sql). The manual recovery of the
-- owner's factors in docs/infrastructure.md deletes their rows without
-- setting `factor_reset_at`, so it is left to work.

-- +goose Up
ALTER TABLE account
    ADD COLUMN deactivated_at timestamptz,
    ADD COLUMN factor_reset_at timestamptz,
    ADD CONSTRAINT account_only_staff_are_offboarded
        CHECK (kind = 'staff' OR (deactivated_at IS NULL AND factor_reset_at IS NULL));

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE FUNCTION account_keeps_the_owner() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE account_id = NEW.id AND owner) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'the owner is not deactivated, and their second factor is not reset by anybody else'
            USING ERRCODE = 'restrict_violation';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

CREATE TRIGGER account_keeps_the_owner
    BEFORE UPDATE OF deactivated_at, factor_reset_at ON account
    FOR EACH ROW
    WHEN (NEW.deactivated_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.deactivated_at
          OR NEW.factor_reset_at IS DISTINCT FROM OLD.factor_reset_at)
    EXECUTE FUNCTION account_keeps_the_owner();

-- +goose Down
DROP TRIGGER IF EXISTS account_keeps_the_owner ON account;
DROP FUNCTION IF EXISTS account_keeps_the_owner();
ALTER TABLE account
    DROP CONSTRAINT IF EXISTS account_only_staff_are_offboarded,
    DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS factor_reset_at,
    DROP COLUMN IF EXISTS deactivated_at;
```

```go
var (
	// ErrStaffUnknown is a change to an account that is not a staff
	// member's: unknown, malformed, or a buyer's, which the platform's
	// transaction does not see.
	ErrStaffUnknown = errors.New("identity: no such staff member")
	// ErrDeactivated is a change to a staff account that was deactivated.
	ErrDeactivated = errors.New("identity: the staff account is deactivated")
)

// StaffChange is what a change a colleague makes to a staff account runs in
// the change's own transaction, given the account, locked (F15 spec, D9):
// Check first, before anything changes, whose error refuses the change;
// Then last, before the notice, which records who made it. Both are the
// console's to say: who may change whom, and the audit with the colleague
// as its actor. By is the colleague's name, which the notice tells the
// account's owner.
type StaffChange struct {
	Check func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, target Account) error
	Then  func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, target Account) error
	By    string
}

// ResetFactors removes every second factor of the staff account target, its
// recovery codes and its enrolments in progress, ends its sign-ins waiting
// for a second step and every session it has, and tells its owner by
// e-mail. The next sign-in, with the password, opens a session with no
// second factor, which the console sends to enrol a new one before anything
// else (F15 spec, D9; D4). The account's row records when, which the
// database refuses for the owner (migrations/00021_staff_offboarding.sql).
//
// The rows go in the order every path takes them — challenges, factors,
// sessions, the audit chain, the failure count last — so a reset never
// waits on a sign-in that waits on it.
func (s *Service) ResetFactors(ctx context.Context, v Visit, target string, change StaffChange) error {
	return s.changeStaff(ctx, v, target, change, "second-factor-reset", func(tx pgx.Tx, account string, now time.Time) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE account SET factor_reset_at = $2 WHERE id = $1`, account, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := forget(ctx, tx, account, resetRows); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return revokeSessions(ctx, tx, account, "factor_reset", now)
	}, clearFailures)
}

// resetRows are what a reset deletes: the account's challenges, its
// enrolments in progress, its codes by e-mail, its second factors and its
// recovery codes, in that order.
var resetRows = []string{
	`DELETE FROM sign_in_challenge WHERE account_id = $1`,
	`DELETE FROM factor_enrolment WHERE account_id = $1`,
	`DELETE FROM email_code WHERE account_id = $1`,
	`DELETE FROM second_factor WHERE account_id = $1`,
	`DELETE FROM recovery_code WHERE account_id = $1`,
}

// Deactivate deactivates the staff account target: it signs in no more, and
// a sign-in with it is answered as a wrong password is; its sign-ins waiting
// for a second step, its enrolments in progress and every session end; and
// its owner is told by e-mail (F15 spec, D9). Its second factors are kept,
// for the reactivation a later delivery brings. The database refuses it for
// the owner (migrations/00021_staff_offboarding.sql).
func (s *Service) Deactivate(ctx context.Context, v Visit, target string, change StaffChange) error {
	return s.changeStaff(ctx, v, target, change, "staff-deactivated", func(tx pgx.Tx, account string, now time.Time) error {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `UPDATE account SET deactivated_at = $2 WHERE id = $1`, account, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := forget(ctx, tx, account, deactivatedRows); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return revokeSessions(ctx, tx, account, "deactivated", now)
	}, nil)
}

// deactivatedRows are what a deactivation deletes: the account's challenges
// and its enrolments in progress.
var deactivatedRows = []string{
	`DELETE FROM sign_in_challenge WHERE account_id = $1`,
	`DELETE FROM factor_enrolment WHERE account_id = $1`,
}

// forget runs each of statements, which delete an account's rows, for
// account.
func forget(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account string, statements []string) error {
	for _, statement := range statements {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, statement, account); err != nil {
			return err
		}
	}
	return nil
}

// changeStaff runs a colleague's change to the staff account target on a
// platform visit, in one transaction: the account locked and read, the
// console's check, the change, the console's record, the notice, and last
// what must come last (the failure count, whose row every path takes last).
func (s *Service) changeStaff(ctx context.Context, v Visit, target string, change StaffChange, notice string,
	apply func(tx pgx.Tx, account string, now time.Time) error, last func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account string) error) error {
	if !v.Platform {
		return errVisitScope
	}
	var parsed pgtype.UUID
	if parsed.Scan(target) != nil {
		return ErrStaffUnknown
	}
	return s.within(ctx, v, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		if err := lockAccount(ctx, tx, target); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		account, err := accountByID(ctx, tx, target)
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) || (err == nil && account.Kind != KindStaff) {
			return ErrStaffUnknown
		}
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if account.DeactivatedAt != nil {
			return ErrDeactivated
		}
		if change.Check != nil {
			if err := change.Check(ctx, tx, account); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if err := apply(tx, account.ID, s.now()); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if change.Then != nil {
			if err := change.Then(ctx, tx, account); err != nil {
				return err
			}
		}
		if err := notify(ctx, tx, v, account, notice, map[string]string{"By": change.By}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if last != nil {
			return last(ctx, tx, account.ID)
		}
		return nil
	})
}
```

```go
// revokeSessions ends every live session of an account because its password
// changed, at now, the service's clock.
func revokeSessions(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account string, now time.Time) error {
	_, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
		UPDATE session SET revoked_at = $2, revoked_reason = 'password_changed'
		 WHERE account_id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL`, account, now)
	return err
}
```

```go
}

// revokeSessions ends every live session of an account, for reason — its
// password changed, a colleague reset its second factor or deactivated it —
// at now, the service's clock.
func revokeSessions(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account, reason string, now time.Time) error {
	_, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
		UPDATE session SET revoked_at = $2, revoked_reason = $3
		 WHERE account_id = $1 AND revoked_at IS NULL`, account, now, reason)
	return err
}
```
