## Task 17: Invitations at the service: sent with a link that works once for seven days, and accepted

D5: a staff member who may invite across the platform gives a name, an address and the roles
the invitee will hold, each with its scope; the invitee is mailed a link, in the inviter's language, that
works once and for seven days, kept as its SHA-256. Accepting it creates the staff account through
`CreateStaff` (F13's password rules, breach check included), assigns the roles as the inviter's and signs
the new member in with no factor yet. The pairs are rows of their own (`staff_invitation_role`), so the
database keeps the same rules as `user_role` — never the owner role, a platform-only role for the whole
platform only — and a role deleted meanwhile is simply not given. One open invitation per address; a new
one replaces it, and invitations that can no longer be used are deleted then. Two acceptances at once make
one account.

**Files:**
- Create: `migrations/00023_staff_invitations.sql`, `internal/staff/invitations.go`, `internal/staff/invitations_integration_test.go`, `web/mail/staff-invitation.en-US.html`, `web/mail/staff-invitation.en-US.txt`, `web/mail/staff-invitation.pt-BR.html`, `web/mail/staff-invitation.pt-BR.txt`
- Modify: `internal/identity/identity.go`, `internal/identity/staff.go`, `internal/staff/roles.go`, `internal/platform/mail/templates_test.go`, `web/locales/en-US.json`, `web/locales/pt-BR.json`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `identity.CreateStaff`, `ErrAddressTaken`, `NewToken`, `NormaliseEmail`, `CheckName`
  (task 3); `staff.Team`, `record` (task 14); `lockRole`, `allows`, `violates`, `ErrPlatformOnly`,
  `ErrOwnerRole`, `user_role_within_reach()` (tasks 9 and 13).
- Produces: migration `00023_staff_invitations.sql` (`staff_invitation`,
  `staff_invitation_one_open_per_address`, `staff_invitation_role`, `staff_invitation_role_by_role`, the
  trigger `staff_invitation_role_within_reach`, `role_permission_within_reach()` counting open invitations);
  `identity.Visit.Link(path string, query url.Values) string` (was the private `link`);
  `staff.InvitationLifetime = 7 * 24 * time.Hour`, `staff.InvitationPath = "/invitation"`; `ErrNoGrant`,
  `ErrAlreadyStaff`, `ErrDeactivatedStaff`, `ErrInvitationInvalid`; `type Grant struct{ Role, Marketplace string }`;
  `type Invitation struct{ Name, Email, InvitedBy string; ExpiresAt time.Time }`;
  `func (t *Team) Invite(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, v identity.Visit, name, email string, grants []Grant) error`
  (audits `staff.invitation_sent`); `func (t *Team) Invitation(ctx context.Context, token string) (Invitation, error)`;
  `func (t *Team) Accept(ctx context.Context, v identity.Visit, token, password string) (string, error)`
  (audits `staff.invitation_accepted`); mail `staff-invitation` (`{{.Name}}`, `{{.Inviter}}`, `{{.Link}}`,
  `{{.Days}}`, `{{.From}}`).

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the failing tests**

Create `internal/staff/invitations_integration_test.go`:

```go
//go:build integration

package staff_test

import (
	"errors"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"sync"
	"testing"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
)

// named is an actor with a name, as the console gives it.
func named(account, name string) staff.Actor {
	actor := by(account)
	actor.Name = name
	return actor
}

// invitationTo is the variables of the last invitation mailed to an
// address, and the token its link carries.
func (tm team) invitationTo(t *testing.T, to string) (map[string]string, string) {
	t.Helper()
	variables := map[string]string{}
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `
			SELECT payload->'Variables' FROM outbox_event
			 WHERE kind = 'email.send' AND payload->>'Template' = 'staff-invitation' AND payload->>'To' = $1
			 ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`, to).Scan(&variables)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("no invitation was mailed to %s: %v", to, err)
	}
	link, err := url.Parse(variables["Link"])
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return variables, link.Query().Get("token")
}

// role makes a role of permissions and returns it.
func (tm team) role(t *testing.T, name string, permissions ...staff.Permission) string {
	t.Helper()
	id, err := tm.roles.Create(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), name, permissions)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	return id
}

// An invitation is sent by who may invite across the platform, mailed with a
// link in the inviter's language, and used once: its page reads who is
// invited and by whom; a password the rules refuse leaves it open; the
// right one makes the staff account, assigns the roles it gives as the
// inviter's, and signs it in with no second factor yet; and then the link
// is no longer valid. Both ends are audited (D5).
func TestAnInvitationIsAcceptedOnce(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	inviter := tm.member(t, "Inviter", "inviter@example.test", staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffInvite)
	viewers := tm.role(t, "Viewers", staff.ConsoleView)
	if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), named(inviter, "Inviter"), platform(), " Nova ", "Nova@Example.test",
		[]staff.Grant{{Role: viewers, Marketplace: tm.one}, {Role: viewers, Marketplace: tm.one}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Invite = %v", err)
	}
	variables, token := tm.invitationTo(t, "Nova@Example.test")
	if variables["Name"] != "Nova" || variables["Inviter"] != "Inviter" || variables["Days"] != "7" ||
		!strings.HasPrefix(variables["Link"], "https://console.test/en-US/invitation?token=") || token == "" {
		t.Fatalf("the invitation mail carries %v", variables)
	}
	invitation, err := tm.team.Invitation(t.Context(), token)
	if err != nil || invitation.Name != "Nova" || invitation.Email != "Nova@Example.test" || invitation.InvitedBy != "Inviter" {
		t.Fatalf("Invitation = %+v, %v", invitation, err)
	}

	if _, err := tm.team.Accept(t.Context(), platform(), token, "short"); !errors.Is(err, identity.ErrPasswordShort) {
		t.Fatalf("Accept with a short password = %v, want ErrPasswordShort", err)
	}
	session, err := tm.team.Accept(t.Context(), platform(), token, password)
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("Accept = %v", err)
	}
	signed, err := tm.service.AuthenticateStaff(t.Context(), session)
	if err != nil || signed.Account.Email != "Nova@Example.test" || signed.Account.Name != "Nova" || signed.SecondFactor {
		t.Fatalf("the new staff member's session is %+v, %v", signed, err)
	}
	account := signed.Account.ID
	tm.allows(t, account, staff.ConsoleView, tm.one, true)
	tm.allows(t, account, staff.ConsoleView, tm.two, false)
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM user_role WHERE account_id = $1 AND assigned_by = '`+inviter+`'`, account); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d roles were assigned by the inviter, want 1", n)
	}

	for what, err := range map[string]error{
		"accepted again": func() error { _, err := tm.team.Accept(t.Context(), platform(), token, password); return err }(),
		"read again":     func() error { _, err := tm.team.Invitation(t.Context(), token); return err }(),
		"a wrong token":  func() error { _, err := tm.team.Invitation(t.Context(), "not-a-token"); return err }(),
	} {
		if !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid) {
			t.Errorf("the invitation %s = %v, want ErrInvitationInvalid", what, err)
		}
	}

	sent, found := tm.trail.find("staff.invitation_sent")
	if !found || sent.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: inviter, Kind: audit.Staff}) || sent.Subject.Kind != "invitation" ||
		sent.Subject.Person != "" || strings.Contains(string(sent.After), "Nova") {
		t.Fatalf("staff.invitation_sent = %+v (found %v)", sent, found)
	}
	if roles, _ := decoded(t, sent.After)["roles"].([]any); len(roles) != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("staff.invitation_sent gives %s", sent.After)
	}
	accepted, found := tm.trail.find("staff.invitation_accepted")
	if !found || accepted.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: account, Kind: audit.Staff}) || accepted.Subject.Person != account ||
		decoded(t, accepted.After)["invitation"] != sent.Subject.ID {
		t.Fatalf("staff.invitation_accepted = %+v (found %v)", accepted, found)
	}
}

// An expired invitation is no longer valid, and makes no account.
func TestAnExpiredInvitationIsRefused(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	viewers := tm.role(t, "Viewers", staff.ConsoleView)
	if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), named(tm.owner, "Owner"), platform(), "Late", "late@example.test",
		[]staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, token := tm.invitationTo(t, "late@example.test")
	if _, err := tm.database.pool.Exec(t.Context(),
		`UPDATE staff_invitation SET expires_at = now() - interval '1 second' WHERE email_normalised = 'late@example.test'`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.team.Invitation(t.Context(), token); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("an expired invitation's page = %v, want ErrInvitationInvalid", err)
	}
	if _, err := tm.team.Accept(t.Context(), platform(), token, password); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid) {
		t.Fatalf("an expired invitation accepted = %v, want ErrInvitationInvalid", err)
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM account WHERE email_normalised = $1`, "late@example.test"); n != 0 {
		t.Fatal("an expired invitation made an account")
	}
}

// Two acceptances of one invitation at once make one account.
func TestTwoAcceptancesAtOnceMakeOneAccount(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), named(tm.owner, "Owner"), platform(), "Twice", "twice@example.test",
		[]staff.Grant{{Role: tm.role(t, "Viewers", staff.ConsoleView)}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	_, token := tm.invitationTo(t, "twice@example.test")
	errs := make([]error, 2)
	var wg sync.WaitGroup
	for i := range errs {
		wg.Add(1)
		go func() {
			defer wg.Done()
			_, errs[i] = tm.team.Accept(t.Context(), platform(), token, password)
		}()
	}
	wg.Wait()
	accepted, refused := 0, 0
	for _, err := range errs {
		switch {
		case err == nil:
			accepted++
		case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid):
			refused++
		default:
			t.Fatalf("a concurrent Accept = %v", err)
		}
	}
	if accepted != 1 || refused != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("%d acceptances succeeded and %d were refused, want one each", accepted, refused)
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM user_role u JOIN account a ON a.id = u.account_id WHERE a.email_normalised = $1`,
		"twice@example.test"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("the invitation gave %d roles, want 1", n)
	}
}

// What an invitation cannot give is refused before it is sent: no role, the
// owner role, a platform-only role for one marketplace, a marketplace that
// does not exist, an address that is a staff member's — active or
// deactivated —, and anything by who may not invite across the platform. A
// new invitation to an address replaces the open one.
func TestAnInvitationIsRefusedWhatItCannotGive(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	viewers := tm.role(t, "Viewers", staff.ConsoleView)
	managers := tm.role(t, "Managers", staff.ConsoleView, staff.RolesManage)
	deactivated := tm.newStaff(t, "Gone", "gone@example.test")
	if err := tm.team.Deactivate(t.Context(), named(tm.owner, "Owner"), platform(), deactivated); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	local := tm.newStaff(t, "Local", "local@example.test")
	inviters := tm.role(t, "Inviters", staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffInvite)
	if err := tm.roles.Assign(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), inviters, local, tm.one); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrPlatformOnly) {
		t.Fatalf("inviting held for one marketplace = %v, want ErrPlatformOnly", err)
	}

	owner := named(tm.owner, "Owner")
	for _, refused := range []struct {
		actor  staff.Actor
		email  string
		grants []staff.Grant
		want   error
	}{
		{owner, "a@example.test", nil, staff.ErrNoGrant},
		{owner, "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: tm.ownerRole(t)}}, staff.ErrOwnerRole},
		{owner, "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: managers, Marketplace: tm.one}}, staff.ErrPlatformOnly},
		{owner, "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers, Marketplace: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009"}}, staff.ErrScopeUnknown},
		{owner, "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: "00000000-0000-0000-0000-000000000009"}}, staff.ErrRoleNotFound},
		{owner, "Colleague@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}, staff.ErrAlreadyStaff},
		{owner, "gone@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}, staff.ErrDeactivatedStaff},
		{owner, "not an address", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}, identity.ErrEmailInvalid},
		{named(tm.colleague, "Colleague"), "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}, staff.ErrNotAllowed},
		{named(local, "Local"), "a@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}, staff.ErrNotAllowed},
	} {
		if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), refused.actor, platform(), "Someone", refused.email, refused.grants); !errors.Is(err, refused.want) {
			t.Errorf("an invitation to %s with %v by %s = %v, want %v", refused.email, refused.grants, refused.actor.Account, err, refused.want)
		}
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM staff_invitation WHERE $1 <> ''`, "any"); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the refused invitations left %d rows", n)
	}

	for range 2 {
		if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), owner, platform(), "Again", "again@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: viewers}}); err != nil {
			t.Fatal(err)
		}
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM staff_invitation WHERE email_normalised = $1`, "again@example.test"); n != 1 {
		t.Fatalf("two invitations to one address left %d open, want the last", n)
	}
}

// The database gives the same answers to SQL of its own: an invitation
// never offers the owner role, nor a platform-only role for one marketplace,
// and a role an open invitation offers for one marketplace is given no
// platform-only permission, which the service refuses too.
func TestTheDatabaseKeepsWhatAnInvitationOffers(t *testing.T) {
	tm := newTeam(t)
	viewers := tm.role(t, "Viewers", staff.ConsoleView)
	managers := tm.role(t, "Managers", staff.ConsoleView, staff.RolesManage)
	if err := tm.team.Invite(t.Context(), named(tm.owner, "Owner"), platform(), "Nova", "nova@example.test",
		[]staff.Grant{{Role: viewers, Marketplace: tm.one}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	var invitation string
	if err := tm.database.InTx(t.Context(), func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		return tx.QueryRow(t.Context(), `SELECT id::text FROM staff_invitation`).Scan(&invitation)
	}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	for _, refused := range []struct {
		code, statement string
		args            []any
	}{
		{"23503", `INSERT INTO staff_invitation_role (invitation_id, role_id) VALUES ($1, $2)`, []any{invitation, tm.ownerRole(t)}},
		{"23514", `INSERT INTO staff_invitation_role (invitation_id, role_id, owner) VALUES ($1, $2, true)`, []any{invitation, tm.ownerRole(t)}},
		{"23514", `INSERT INTO staff_invitation_role (invitation_id, role_id, marketplace_id) VALUES ($1, $2, $3)`, []any{invitation, managers, tm.one}},
		{"23514", `INSERT INTO role_permission (role_id, permission_key) VALUES ($1, 'staff.invite')`, []any{viewers}},
	} {
		wantRefused(t, tm.database, refused.code, refused.statement, refused.args...)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Edit(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers, "Viewers", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffInvite}); !errors.Is(err, staff.ErrHeldInAMarketplace) {
		t.Fatalf("a platform-only permission for a role offered for one marketplace = %v, want ErrHeldInAMarketplace", err)
	}
	if err := tm.roles.Delete(t.Context(), by(tm.owner), viewers); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("deleting a role an invitation offers = %v", err)
	}
	if n := tm.count(t, `SELECT count(*) FROM staff_invitation_role WHERE invitation_id = $1`, invitation); n != 0 {
		t.Fatalf("the deleted role is still offered %d times", n)
	}
}
```

```go
// The invitation exists in both languages and both parts, names the
// invitee, who invited them and the console, carries the link and says how
// long it works (F15 spec, D5).
func TestTheInvitationCarriesItsLinkAndItsLifetime(t *testing.T) {
	for _, tc := range []struct{ language, days string }{{"en-US", "expires in 7 days"}, {"pt-BR", "expira em 7 dias"}} {
		rendered, err := templates(t).Render(mail.Message{
			Template: "staff-invitation", Language: tc.language, To: "reader@example.test", From: "Console",
			Variables: map[string]string{"Name": "Leitora", "Inviter": "Dona", "Days": "7",
				"Link": "https://console.example/" + tc.language + "/invitation?token=sample"},
		}, i18n.Default)
		if err != nil {
			t.Fatalf("staff-invitation in %s: %v", tc.language, err)
		}
		if rendered.Language != tc.language || !strings.Contains(rendered.Subject, "Dona") || !strings.Contains(rendered.Subject, "Console") {
			t.Errorf("staff-invitation in %s: language %s, subject %q", tc.language, rendered.Language, rendered.Subject)
		}
		for _, part := range []string{rendered.Text, rendered.HTML} {
			for _, want := range []string{"Leitora", "Dona", "Console", "/invitation?token=sample", tc.days} {
				if !strings.Contains(part, want) {
					t.Errorf("staff-invitation in %s does not say %q: %s", tc.language, want, part)
				}
			}
		}
	}
}
```

Create `migrations/00023_staff_invitations.sql`:

```sql
-- Staff join by invitation (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md, D5).
--
-- A staff member who may invite across the platform gives a name, an
-- address and the roles the invitee will hold, each with its scope; the
-- invitee receives a link, valid for seven days and usable once, where they
-- set their own password and then enrol an app or a key. The token is kept
-- as its SHA-256 only, like every other token here.
--
-- The roles and scopes are rows of their own, not a column: a role deleted
-- before the invitation is used, or a marketplace that goes, leaves the
-- invitation with it (ON DELETE CASCADE) rather than naming what is not
-- there; the owner role can never be offered, by the same foreign key on
-- (role_id, owner) that keeps it off `user_role` (migrations/00019_roles.sql)
-- and a check that the row is not the owner's; and a role granting a
-- permission of the whole platform only is offered for the whole platform
-- only, by the trigger that refuses it on `user_role`
-- (migrations/00020_permission_reach.sql), which now also refuses such a
-- permission to a role an open invitation offers for one marketplace.
--
-- At most one invitation is open per address: a new one replaces it. Once
-- used, an invitation stays as the record of the account it made
-- (`account_id`). Both tables belong to the platform.

-- +goose Up
CREATE TABLE staff_invitation (
    id               uuid PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid(),
    token_hash       bytea       NOT NULL CONSTRAINT staff_invitation_token_is_unique UNIQUE,
    email            text        NOT NULL,
    email_normalised text        NOT NULL,
    name             text        NOT NULL,
    invited_by       uuid REFERENCES account (id) ON DELETE SET NULL,
    created_at       timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
    expires_at       timestamptz NOT NULL,
    used_at          timestamptz,
    -- The staff account the invitation made, once used.
    account_id       uuid REFERENCES account (id) ON DELETE SET NULL
);
CREATE UNIQUE INDEX staff_invitation_one_open_per_address ON staff_invitation (email_normalised) WHERE used_at IS NULL;

CREATE TABLE staff_invitation_role (
    invitation_id  uuid    NOT NULL REFERENCES staff_invitation (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    role_id        uuid    NOT NULL,
    owner          boolean NOT NULL DEFAULT false
        CONSTRAINT staff_invitation_role_is_not_the_owners CHECK (NOT owner),
    -- The scope: one marketplace, or NULL for the whole platform.
    marketplace_id uuid REFERENCES marketplace (id) ON DELETE CASCADE,

    CONSTRAINT staff_invitation_role_is_a_role FOREIGN KEY (role_id, owner)
        REFERENCES role (id, owner) ON DELETE CASCADE,
    CONSTRAINT staff_invitation_role_is_unique UNIQUE NULLS NOT DISTINCT (invitation_id, role_id, marketplace_id)
);
CREATE INDEX staff_invitation_role_by_role ON staff_invitation_role (role_id);

ALTER TABLE staff_invitation ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;
ALTER TABLE staff_invitation_role ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY;

CREATE POLICY staff_invitation_belongs_to_the_platform ON staff_invitation
    FOR ALL USING (current_marketplace_id() IS NULL)
    WITH CHECK (current_marketplace_id() IS NULL);
CREATE POLICY staff_invitation_role_belongs_to_the_platform ON staff_invitation_role
    FOR ALL USING (current_marketplace_id() IS NULL)
    WITH CHECK (current_marketplace_id() IS NULL);

CREATE TRIGGER staff_invitation_role_within_reach
    BEFORE INSERT OR UPDATE OF role_id, marketplace_id ON staff_invitation_role
    FOR EACH ROW EXECUTE FUNCTION user_role_within_reach();

-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION role_permission_within_reach() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM permission WHERE key = NEW.permission_key AND platform_only)
       AND (EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = NEW.role_id AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL)
            OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM staff_invitation_role r JOIN staff_invitation i ON i.id = r.invitation_id
                        WHERE r.role_id = NEW.role_id AND r.marketplace_id IS NOT NULL
                          AND i.used_at IS NULL AND i.expires_at > now())) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'a role held or offered for one marketplace is given no permission of the whole platform'
            USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', CONSTRAINT = 'role_within_reach';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd

-- +goose Down
-- +goose StatementBegin
CREATE OR REPLACE FUNCTION role_permission_within_reach() RETURNS trigger
    LANGUAGE plpgsql
    SET search_path = public, pg_temp
AS $$
BEGIN
    IF EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM permission WHERE key = NEW.permission_key AND platform_only)
       AND EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = NEW.role_id AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL) THEN
        RAISE EXCEPTION 'a role held for one marketplace is given no permission of the whole platform'
            USING ERRCODE = 'check_violation', CONSTRAINT = 'role_within_reach';
    END IF;
    RETURN NEW;
END
$$;
-- +goose StatementEnd
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS staff_invitation_role;
DROP TABLE IF EXISTS staff_invitation;
```

Create `internal/staff/invitations.go`:

```go
package staff

import (
	"context"
	"errors"
	"net/url"
	"slices"
	"strconv"
	"strings"
	"time"

	"github.com/jackc/pgx/v5"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/mail"
)

// InvitationLifetime is how long an invitation's link works (D5).
const InvitationLifetime = 7 * 24 * time.Hour

// InvitationPath is where an invitation's link leads on the console, below
// the language.
const InvitationPath = "/invitation"

var (
	// ErrNoGrant is an invitation that gives no role: its staff member
	// would see nothing of the console.
	ErrNoGrant = errors.New("staff: an invitation gives at least one role")
	// ErrAlreadyStaff is an invitation to an address that belongs to a staff
	// member: their roles are assigned on the role pages instead.
	ErrAlreadyStaff = errors.New("staff: the address belongs to a staff member")
	// ErrDeactivatedStaff is an invitation to the address of a deactivated
	// staff member, whom an invitation cannot bring back: reactivation is
	// not in F15.
	ErrDeactivatedStaff = errors.New("staff: the address belongs to a deactivated staff member")
	// ErrInvitationInvalid is an invitation that is unknown, used or
	// expired: one answer for the three, as for every other token.
	ErrInvitationInvalid = errors.New("staff: the invitation is no longer valid")
)

// Grant is a role an invitation gives, in a scope: one marketplace, or the
// whole platform (Marketplace is Platform).
type Grant struct {
	Role        string
	Marketplace string
}

// Invitation is an open invitation as its page shows it: who is invited,
// and by whom.
type Invitation struct {
	Name      string
	Email     string
	InvitedBy string
	ExpiresAt time.Time
}

// Invite invites name, at email, to the staff with grants, as actor, who
// may invite across the platform, and sends the invitation's link by e-mail
// in the visit's language (D5). An open invitation to the same address is
// replaced: its link stops working. An address that already belongs to a
// staff member is refused, active or deactivated. The owner role is never
// given, and a role granting a permission of the whole platform only is
// given for the whole platform only. The invitation is audited with actor
// as its actor, and says who is invited only through its id: the name and
// the address stay on the invitation, which is deleted once it can no
// longer be used and another is sent.
func (t *Team) Invite(ctx context.Context, actor Actor, v identity.Visit, name, email string, grants []Grant) error {
	name, err := identity.CheckName(name)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	normalised, err := identity.NormaliseEmail(email)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	grants, err = checkGrants(grants)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	token, hash, err := identity.NewToken()
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	if !validID(actor.Account) {
		return ErrNotAllowed
	}
	return t.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		allowed, err := allows(ctx, tx, actor.Account, StaffInvite, Platform)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if !allowed {
			return ErrNotAllowed
		}
		if err := notStaff(ctx, tx, normalised); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		now := t.now()
		// The address's open invitation goes, and with it every invitation
		// no longer usable: they hold people's names and addresses for
		// nothing.
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
			DELETE FROM staff_invitation WHERE used_at IS NULL AND (email_normalised = $1 OR expires_at <= $2)`,
			normalised, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		var id string
		if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO staff_invitation (token_hash, email, email_normalised, name, invited_by, created_at, expires_at)
			VALUES ($1, $2, $3, $4, $5, $6, $7) RETURNING id::text`,
			hash, strings.TrimSpace(email), normalised, name, actor.Account, now, now.Add(InvitationLifetime)).Scan(&id); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		given, err := offer(ctx, tx, id, grants)
		if err != nil {
			return err
		}
		if err := mail.Request(ctx, tx, mail.Message{
			Template: "staff-invitation", Language: v.Language, To: strings.TrimSpace(email), From: v.MarketplaceName,
			Variables: map[string]string{"Name": name, "Inviter": actor.Name,
				"Link": v.Link(InvitationPath, url.Values{"token": {token}}),
				"Days": strconv.Itoa(int(InvitationLifetime / (24 * time.Hour)))},
		}); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		return record(ctx, tx, t.audit, actor, "staff.invitation_sent", audit.Subject{Kind: "invitation", ID: id},
			nil, described{"roles": given})
	})
}

// checkGrants returns the grants, each once, or why they cannot be given.
func checkGrants(grants []Grant) ([]Grant, error) {
	var checked []Grant
	for _, g := range grants {
		if !validID(g.Role) {
			return nil, ErrRoleNotFound
		}
		if g.Marketplace != Platform && !validID(g.Marketplace) {
			return nil, ErrScopeUnknown
		}
		if !slices.Contains(checked, g) {
			checked = append(checked, g)
		}
	}
	if len(checked) == 0 {
		return nil, ErrNoGrant
	}
	return checked, nil
}

// notStaff refuses an address that belongs to a staff member: the
// platform's transaction sees staff accounts and nobody else's.
func notStaff(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, normalised string) error {
	var deactivated *time.Time
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `SELECT deactivated_at FROM account WHERE email_normalised = $1`, normalised).Scan(&deactivated)
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows):
		return nil
	case err != nil:
		return err
	case deactivated != nil:
		return ErrDeactivatedStaff
	}
	return ErrAlreadyStaff
}

// offer writes the roles invitation gives, each role locked against a
// change while it is offered, and returns them as the audit records them.
func offer(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, invitation string, grants []Grant) ([]described, error) {
	var given []described
	for _, g := range grants {
		role, err := lockRole(ctx, tx, g.Role, "FOR SHARE")
		if err != nil {
			return nil, err
		}
		if g.Marketplace != Platform && role.PlatformOnly() {
			return nil, ErrPlatformOnly
		}
		_, err = tx.Exec(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO staff_invitation_role (invitation_id, role_id, marketplace_id) VALUES ($1, $2, nullif($3, '')::uuid)`,
			invitation, g.Role, g.Marketplace)
		switch {
		case violates(err, "staff_invitation_role_marketplace_id_fkey"):
			return nil, ErrScopeUnknown
		case violates(err, "role_within_reach"):
			return nil, ErrPlatformOnly
		case err != nil:
			return nil, err
		}
		given = append(given, described{"role": g.Role, "scope": scopeName(g.Marketplace)})
	}
	return given, nil
}

// Invitation returns the open invitation token opened, or
// ErrInvitationInvalid for one that is unknown, used or expired.
func (t *Team) Invitation(ctx context.Context, token string) (Invitation, error) {
	var found Invitation
	err := t.db.InTx(ctx, func(tx pgx.Tx) error {
		err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
			SELECT i.name, i.email, coalesce(a.name, ''), i.expires_at
			  FROM staff_invitation i LEFT JOIN account a ON a.id = i.invited_by
			 WHERE i.token_hash = $1 AND i.used_at IS NULL AND i.expires_at > $2`,
			identity.HashToken(token), t.now()).Scan(&found.Name, &found.Email, &found.InvitedBy, &found.ExpiresAt)
		if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
			return ErrInvitationInvalid
		}
		return err
	})
	return found, err
}

// Accept uses the invitation token opened: it creates the staff account it
// names, with password under F13's rules, breach check included, gives it
// the invitation's roles — assigned by whoever invited — and signs it in,
// returning the session's token; the console then asks for a second factor
// before anything else (D4, D5). The invitation is used once: two
// acceptances at once make one account, the second finding it used. An
// invitation that is unknown, used or expired is ErrInvitationInvalid, and
// so is one whose address took a staff account meanwhile.
func (t *Team) Accept(ctx context.Context, v identity.Visit, token, password string) (string, error) {
	invitation, err := t.Invitation(ctx, token)
	if err != nil {
		return "", err
	}
	session, err := t.identity.CreateStaff(ctx, v, invitation.Name, invitation.Email, password,
		func(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, account string) error {
			return t.use(ctx, tx, v, identity.HashToken(token), account)
		})
	if errors.Is(err, identity.ErrAddressTaken) {
		return "", ErrInvitationInvalid
	}
	return session, err
}

// use marks the invitation hash names as used by account, in the account's
// own transaction, and assigns account the roles it gives.
func (t *Team) use(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, v identity.Visit, hash []byte, account string) error {
	now := t.now()
	var id string
	var invitedBy *string
	err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		UPDATE staff_invitation SET used_at = $2, account_id = $3
		 WHERE token_hash = $1 AND used_at IS NULL AND expires_at > $2
		RETURNING id::text, invited_by::text`, hash, now, account).Scan(&id, &invitedBy)
	if errors.Is(err, pgx.ErrNoRows) {
		return ErrInvitationInvalid
	}
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	rows, err := tx.Query(ctx, `
		SELECT role_id::text, coalesce(marketplace_id::text, '') FROM staff_invitation_role
		 WHERE invitation_id = $1 ORDER BY role_id, marketplace_id NULLS FIRST`, id)
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	grants, err := pgx.CollectRows(rows, pgx.RowToStructByPos[Grant])
	if err != nil {
		return err
	}
	var given []described
	for _, g := range grants {
		if _, err := tx.Exec(ctx, `
			INSERT INTO user_role (account_id, role_id, marketplace_id, assigned_by, assigned_at)
			VALUES ($1, $2, nullif($3, '')::uuid, $4, $5)`, account, g.Role, g.Marketplace, invitedBy, now); err != nil {
			return err
		}
		given = append(given, described{"role": g.Role, "scope": scopeName(g.Marketplace)})
	}
	return record(ctx, tx, t.audit, Actor{Account: account, IP: v.IP, UserAgent: v.UserAgent}, "staff.invitation_accepted",
		personal(account), nil, described{"invitation": id, "roles": given})
}
```

```go
// notHeldInAMarketplace refuses, with ErrHeldInAMarketplace, a role
// somebody holds for one marketplace: it cannot be given a permission of the
// whole platform only.
func notHeldInAMarketplace(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, role string) error {
	var held bool
	if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = $1 AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL)`, role).Scan(&held); err != nil {
		return err
	}
```

with:

```go

// notHeldInAMarketplace refuses, with ErrHeldInAMarketplace, a role
// somebody holds for one marketplace, or an open invitation offers for one:
// it cannot be given a permission of the whole platform only.
func notHeldInAMarketplace(ctx context.Context, tx pgx.Tx, role string, now time.Time) error {
	var held bool
	if err := tx.QueryRow(ctx, `
		SELECT EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM user_role WHERE role_id = $1 AND marketplace_id IS NOT NULL)
		    OR EXISTS (SELECT 1 FROM staff_invitation_role r JOIN staff_invitation i ON i.id = r.invitation_id
		                WHERE r.role_id = $1 AND r.marketplace_id IS NOT NULL
		                  AND i.used_at IS NULL AND i.expires_at > $2)`, role, now).Scan(&held); err != nil {
		return err
	}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Commit**

```bash
git add -A internal/identity internal/platform/mail internal/staff migrations web/locales web/mail
git commit -F - <<'EOF'
Invitations at the service: sent with a link that works once for seven days, and accepted

Staff join by invitation (F15 spec, D5). A staff member who may invite
across the platform gives a name, an address and the roles the invitee
will hold, each with its scope; the invitee is mailed a link, in the
inviter's language, that works once and for seven days. Its token is kept
as its SHA-256 only. Accepting it creates the staff account with a
password under F13's rules, breach check included, assigns the roles as
the inviter's, and signs the new member in with no second factor yet,
which the console asks for before anything else. An unknown, used or
expired invitation is one answer: no longer valid. Two acceptances at once
make one account.

An invitation gives at least one role, never the owner role, and a role
granting a permission of the whole platform only is given for the whole
platform only; the pairs are rows of their own, so the database keeps the
same rules and a role deleted meanwhile is no longer offered. An address
that is already a staff member's is refused, active or deactivated: an
active member's roles are assigned on the role pages, and reactivation is
not in F15. A new invitation replaces the open one to the same address, and
invitations that can no longer be used are deleted then, since they hold a
name and an address for nothing. Both ends are audited, the invitation by
its id rather than by who is invited.
EOF
```


## Task 18: The invitation's pages: invited from the staff list, joined through the link under its own limit

The spec's flow "Invitation": the staff list offers the form to whoever may invite across the
platform — a name, an address, and the roles with where each applies (the whole platform alone for a
platform-only role) — behind the step-up (D10), each refusal on its field with what was typed and ticked
kept. The link opens a page on the console's host naming who is invited and by whom, where the invitee
sets their password and is signed in and sent to enrol, as the owner was at the first run. A used,
expired or replaced link is "no longer valid" (410). Acceptances are limited per client address under
their own named limit, `invitation`.

Create `internal/platform/httpx/invitation_integration_test.go`:

```go
//go:build integration

package httpx_test

import (
	"net/http"
	"net/http/httptest"
	"net/url"
	"strings"
	"testing"

	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/identity"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/audit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/platform/ratelimit"
	"github.com/aleogr/marketplace/internal/staff"
)

// invitationLink is the path and query of the link the last invitation to
// an address carries, as the console serves it below the language.
func (c console) invitationLink(t *testing.T, to string) string {
	t.Helper()
	var link string
	if err := c.pool.QueryRow(t.Context(), `
		SELECT payload->'Variables'->>'Link' FROM outbox_event
		 WHERE kind = 'email.send' AND payload->>'Template' = 'staff-invitation' AND payload->>'To' = $1
		 ORDER BY created_at DESC, id DESC LIMIT 1`, to).Scan(&link); err != nil {
		t.Fatalf("no invitation was mailed to %s: %v", to, err)
	}
	parsed, err := url.Parse(link)
	if err != nil || !strings.HasPrefix(parsed.Path, "/pt-BR/") {
		t.Fatalf("the invitation's link is %q (%v)", link, err)
	}
	return strings.TrimPrefix(parsed.Path, "/pt-BR") + "?" + parsed.RawQuery
}

// An invitation from the console, accepted through its link: the form asks
// for the second factor again first; a refusal is said on its field with
// what was typed kept; the invitation is mailed; its link opens the page
// naming who is invited and by whom, where a password the rules refuse is
// said on its field and the right one signs the new member in, to add a
// factor first; the new member then sees only what their role permits; and
// the link is no longer valid. Both ends are audited (D5, D8, D10).
func TestAnInvitationIsAcceptedThroughItsLink(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	owner, key := c.signedInOwner(t)
	role, err := c.roles.Create(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), "Leitura", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantStatus(t, owner.get("/staff"), http.StatusOK, `href="/pt-BR/staff/invite"`)
	wantRedirect(t, owner.get("/staff/invite"), "/pt-BR/account/verify?for=staff&next=%2Fstaff%2Finvite")
	stepUpOnConsole(t, owner, key, "/staff/invite")
	form := owner.get("/staff/invite")
	for _, want := range []string{`name="grant" value="` + role + `"`, `name="scope-` + role + `"`,
		`<option value="` + c.marketplace.ID + `">Loja</option>`, "funciona uma vez e por 7 dias"} {
		if !strings.Contains(form.Body.String(), want) {
			t.Fatalf("the invitation's form (status %d) does not hold %q: %s", form.Code, want, form.Body.String())
		}
	}

	noRole := owner.post("/staff/invite", url.Values{"name": {"Nova"}, "email": {"nova@example.test"}})
	wantStatus(t, noRole, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Escolha pelo menos um papel.")
	if !strings.Contains(noRole.Body.String(), `value="nova@example.test"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refused form lost the address: %s", noRole.Body.String())
	}
	taken := owner.post("/staff/invite", url.Values{"name": {"Nova"}, "email": {ownerEmail}, "grant": {role}})
	wantStatus(t, taken, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Este endereço já é de um membro da equipe")
	if !strings.Contains(input(t, taken.Body.String(), "email"), `aria-invalid="true"`) ||
		!strings.Contains(taken.Body.String(), `value="`+role+`" checked`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refusal is not on the address, or lost the roles: %s", taken.Body.String())
	}
	wantRedirect(t, owner.post("/staff/invite", url.Values{"name": {"Nova"}, "email": {"nova@example.test"},
		"grant": {role}, "scope-" + role: {c.marketplace.ID}}), "/pt-BR/staff?done=invited")
	wantStatus(t, owner.get("/staff?done=invited"), http.StatusOK, "O convite foi enviado.")
	if entry, ok := c.trail.find("staff.invitation_sent"); !ok || entry.Actor != (audit.Actor{ID: c.ownerID(t), Kind: audit.Staff}) {
		t.Fatalf("staff.invitation_sent = %+v (found %v)", entry, ok)
	}

	link := c.invitationLink(t, "nova@example.test")
	newcomer := c.onConsole(t)
	page := newcomer.get(link)
	wantStatus(t, page, http.StatusOK, "Dona convidou você, Nova, para a equipe do console, com o endereço nova@example.test.")
	token := strings.TrimPrefix(link, "/invitation?token=")
	short := newcomer.post("/invitation", url.Values{"token": {token}, "password": {"short"}})
	wantStatus(t, short, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, "Dona convidou você")
	if !strings.Contains(input(t, short.Body.String(), "password"), `aria-invalid="true"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the refusal is not on the password: %s", short.Body.String())
	}
	wantRedirect(t, newcomer.post("/invitation", url.Values{"token": {token}, "password": {ownerPassword}}), "/pt-BR/enrol")
	wantRedirect(t, newcomer.get("/"), "/pt-BR/enrol")
	enrolAppOnConsole(t, newcomer)
	home := newcomer.get("/")
	if home.Code != http.StatusOK || !strings.Contains(home.Body.String(), "Nova") ||
		strings.Contains(home.Body.String(), `href="/pt-BR/roles"`) || strings.Contains(home.Body.String(), `href="/pt-BR/staff"`) {
		t.Fatalf("the new member's home: status %d, body %s", home.Code, home.Body.String())
	}
	for _, path := range []string{"/roles", "/staff", "/staff/invite", "/console/version"} {
		if answer := newcomer.get(path); answer.Code != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("%s for the new member = %d, want 404", path, answer.Code)
		}
	}
	if entry, ok := c.trail.find("staff.invitation_accepted"); !ok || entry.Actor.Kind != audit.Staff || entry.Actor.ID == c.ownerID(t) {
		t.Fatalf("staff.invitation_accepted = %+v (found %v)", entry, ok)
	}

	for _, again := range []*httptest.ResponseRecorder{
		c.onConsole(t).get(link),
		c.onConsole(t).post("/invitation", url.Values{"token": {token}, "password": {ownerPassword}}),
	} {
		wantStatus(t, again, http.StatusGone, "Este convite não é mais válido")
	}
}

// An expired invitation's link is no longer valid, on its page and when its
// form is sent anyway.
func TestAnExpiredInvitationIsNoLongerValid(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	role, err := c.roles.Create(t.Context(), c.asOwner(t), "Leitura", []staff.Permission{staff.ConsoleView})
	if err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	owner := c.asOwner(t)
	owner.Name = "Dona"
	v := identity.Visit{Platform: true, Language: "pt-BR", MarketplaceName: "Console", BaseURL: "http://console.test"}
	if err := c.team.Invite(t.Context(), owner, v, "Tarde", "tarde@example.test", []staff.Grant{{Role: role}}); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	link := c.invitationLink(t, "tarde@example.test")
	if _, err := c.pool.Exec(t.Context(), `UPDATE staff_invitation SET expires_at = now() - interval '1 second'`); err != nil {
		t.Fatal(err)
	}
	wantStatus(t, c.onConsole(t).get(link), http.StatusGone, "Este convite não é mais válido")
	wantStatus(t, c.onConsole(t).post("/invitation", url.Values{"token": {strings.TrimPrefix(link, "/invitation?token=")},
		"password": {ownerPassword}}), http.StatusGone, "Este convite não é mais válido")
	wantStatus(t, c.onConsole(t).get("/invitation?token=not-a-token"), http.StatusGone, "Este convite não é mais válido")
}

// Every acceptance counts against the invitations' own named limit.
func TestAcceptingInvitationsIsLimited(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsoleWith(t, true, bootToken, ratelimit.Never{}, refusing{})
	if refused := c.onConsole(t).post("/invitation", url.Values{"token": {"not-a-token"}, "password": {ownerPassword}}); refused.Code != http.StatusTooManyRequests {
		t.Fatalf("an acceptance over its limit = %d, want 429", refused.Code)
	}
}

// The invitation's form is "not found" to a staff member who may not invite
// across the platform, and the staff list offers it to nobody else.
func TestTheInvitationIsNotFoundWithoutItsPermission(t *testing.T) {
	c := newConsole(t, true)
	resetter, account, _ := c.colleague(t, "resetter@example.test")
	c.grant(t, account, staff.Platform, staff.ConsoleView, staff.StaffResetFactor)
	if list := resetter.get("/staff"); list.Code != http.StatusOK || strings.Contains(list.Body.String(), "/staff/invite") {
		t.Fatalf("the staff list for who may not invite: status %d, body %s", list.Code, list.Body.String())
	}
	for _, answer := range []int{resetter.get("/staff/invite").Code,
		resetter.post("/staff/invite", url.Values{"name": {"Nova"}, "email": {"nova@example.test"}}).Code} {
		if answer != http.StatusNotFound {
			t.Errorf("the invitation for who may not invite = %d, want 404", answer)
		}
	}
}
```

```go
// Offer is a role an invitation may give: its id and name, whether it is
// ticked and where it applies, and whether it reaches the whole platform
// only, in which case it applies there and nowhere else.
type Offer struct {
	ID           string
	Name         string
	Checked      bool
	Scope        string
	PlatformOnly bool
}

// Invite is the invitation's form: the name and address typed, in Form
// with its refusal; the roles it may give; the marketplaces a role may be
// given in; and how many days the link works.
type Invite struct {
	Form   Form
	Offers []Offer
	Scopes []Option
	Days   int
}

// ScopeField is the name of the list choosing where an offered role
// applies.
func ScopeField(offer Offer) string { return "scope-" + offer.ID }

// Joining is the page an invitation's link opens: who is invited and by
// whom, the token the form sends back, and the password's form; Invalid
// when the link is no longer valid.
type Joining struct {
	Name      string
	Email     string
	InvitedBy string
	Token     string
	Invalid   bool
	Form      Form
}
```

```templ
// InviteForm invites a staff member (F15 spec, D5): their name and address,
// and the roles they will hold, each where it applies — the whole platform,
// or one marketplace when the role reaches that far.
templ InviteForm(page Page, view Invite) {
	@Layout(page, page.T("console.invite.title")) {
		<h1>{ page.T("console.invite.title") }</h1>
		<p>{ page.T("console.invite.intro", view.Days) }</p>
		@refusal(view.Form, page)
		<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/staff/invite") }>
			<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
			<label>
				{ page.T("identity.field.name") }
				<input
					name="name"
					autocomplete="off"
					required
					maxlength={ strconv.Itoa(view.Form.NameMaxLength) }
					value={ view.Form.Name }
					{ view.Form.Input("name")... }
				/>
			</label>
			<label>
				{ page.T("identity.field.email") }
				<input name="email" type="email" autocomplete="off" required value={ view.Form.Email } { view.Form.Input("email")... }/>
			</label>
			<fieldset class="choices">
				<legend>{ page.T("console.invite.roles") }</legend>
				if len(view.Offers) == 0 {
					<p>{ page.T("console.invite.no_roles") }</p>
				}
				for _, offer := range view.Offers {
					<div class="offer">
						<label class="choice">
							<input type="checkbox" name="grant" value={ offer.ID } checked?={ offer.Checked }/>
							<span>{ offer.Name }</span>
						</label>
						<select name={ ScopeField(offer) } aria-label={ page.T("console.invite.scope_named", offer.Name) }>
							<option value="">{ page.T("console.roles.everywhere") }</option>
							if !offer.PlatformOnly {
								for _, scope := range view.Scopes {
									<option value={ scope.Value } selected?={ scope.Value == offer.Scope }>{ scope.Label }</option>
								}
							}
						</select>
					</div>
				}
			</fieldset>
			<button type="submit">{ page.T("console.invite.submit") }</button>
		</form>
		<p><a href={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/staff") }>{ page.T("console.staff.back") }</a></p>
	}
}

// JoiningPage is where an invitation's link leads: the invitee sets their
// password, under F13's rules, and goes on to add an app or a key; or reads
// that the link is no longer valid (F15 spec, D5).
templ JoiningPage(page Page, view Joining) {
	@Layout(page, page.T("console.invitation.title")) {
		<h1>{ page.T("console.invitation.title") }</h1>
		if view.Invalid {
			<p id={ ErrorID } role="alert">{ page.T("console.invitation.invalid") }</p>
		} else {
			<p>{ page.T("console.invitation.intro", view.InvitedBy, view.Name, view.Email) }</p>
			@refusal(view.Form, page)
			<form method="post" action={ templ.SafeURL("/" + page.Language + "/invitation") }>
				<input type="hidden" name="csrf_token" value={ page.CSRFToken }/>
				<input type="hidden" name="token" value={ view.Token }/>
				<input type="hidden" name="username" autocomplete="username" value={ view.Email }/>
				<label>
					{ page.T("identity.field.password") }
					<input
						name="password"
						type="password"
						autocomplete="new-password"
						required
						minlength={ strconv.Itoa(view.Form.MinLength) }
						maxlength={ strconv.Itoa(view.Form.MaxLength) }
						{ view.Form.Input("password", PasswordHintID)... }
					/>
				</label>
				<p id={ PasswordHintID }>{ page.T("identity.field.password_hint", view.Form.MinLength) }</p>
				<button type="submit">{ page.T("console.invitation.submit") }</button>
			</form>
		}
	}
}
```

```go
// inviteFields are the field each refusal of the invitation's form is
// about, by the key it is shown as.
var inviteFields = map[string]string{
	"identity.error.name_missing":        "name",
	"identity.error.name_invalid":        "name",
	"identity.error.email_invalid":       "email",
	"console.invite.error.already_staff": "email",
	"console.invite.error.deactivated":   "email",
	"console.invite.error.no_role":       "",
	"console.invite.error.role_unknown":  "",
	"console.roles.error.platform_only":  "",
	"console.roles.error.scope_unknown":  "",
}

// inviteRefusal is the key an invitation's refusal is shown as, with its
// arguments; false for an error that is not a refusal the form says.
func inviteRefusal(err error) (string, []any, bool) {
	if key, args, shown := formError(err); shown {
		return key, args, true
	}
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNoGrant):
		return "console.invite.error.no_role", nil, true
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrAlreadyStaff):
		return "console.invite.error.already_staff", nil, true
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrDeactivatedStaff):
		return "console.invite.error.deactivated", nil, true
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrRoleNotFound), errors.Is(err, staff.ErrOwnerRole):
		return "console.invite.error.role_unknown", nil, true
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrPlatformOnly):
		return "console.roles.error.platform_only", nil, true
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrScopeUnknown):
		return "console.roles.error.scope_unknown", nil, true
	}
	return "", nil, false
}

// showInvite renders the invitation's form, with what was typed and ticked
// when a post was refused.
func (s Site) showInvite(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, form web.Form, grants []staff.Grant) {
	roles, err := s.console.Roles.List(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "the roles could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	scopes, err := s.console.Roles.Scopes(r.Context())
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "the marketplaces could not be read", err)
		return
	}
	view := web.Invite{Form: form, Days: int(staff.InvitationLifetime.Hours() / 24)}
	for _, role := range roles {
		if role.Owner {
			continue
		}
		offer := web.Offer{ID: role.ID, Name: role.Name, PlatformOnly: role.PlatformOnly()}
		for _, g := range grants {
			if g.Role == role.ID {
				offer.Checked, offer.Scope = true, g.Marketplace
			}
		}
		view.Offers = append(view.Offers, offer)
	}
	for _, scope := range scopes {
		view.Scopes = append(view.Scopes, web.Option{Value: scope.ID, Label: scope.Name})
	}
	renderStatus(w, r, status, web.InviteForm(s.page(r, invitePath), view))
}

func (s Site) inviteForm(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, invitePath) {
		return
	}
	s.showInvite(w, r, http.StatusOK, newForm(), nil)
}

// invite sends an invitation from its form, and goes back to the staff
// list saying so; a refusal is said on the field it is about, with what was
// typed and ticked kept.
func (s Site) invite(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	if s.stepUpFirst(w, r, invitePath) {
		return
	}
	_ = r.ParseForm()
	var grants []staff.Grant
	for _, role := range r.PostForm["grant"] {
		grants = append(grants, staff.Grant{Role: role, Marketplace: r.PostFormValue("scope-" + role)})
	}
	form := newForm()
	form.Name, form.Email = r.PostFormValue("name"), r.PostFormValue("email")
	err := s.console.Team.Invite(r.Context(), actor(r), visit(r), form.Name, form.Email, grants)
	if key, args, refused := inviteRefusal(err); refused {
		form.Error, form.ErrorArgs, form.Field = key, args, inviteFields[key]
		s.showInvite(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, form, grants)
		return
	}
	switch {
	case errors.Is(err, staff.ErrNotAllowed):
		http.NotFound(w, r)
	case err != nil:
		s.failed(w, r, "an invitation could not be sent", err)
	default:
		http.Redirect(w, r, "/"+i18n.FromContext(r.Context())+staffPath+"?done=invited", http.StatusSeeOther)
	}
}

// showJoining renders the page an invitation's link opens, for token.
func (s Site) showJoining(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, status int, view web.Joining) {
	page := s.page(r, staff.InvitationPath)
	if view.Token != "" {
		// The page is the link's, token and all, whichever language it is
		// switched to.
		page.Query = url.Values{"token": {view.Token}}.Encode()
	}
	renderStatus(w, r, status, web.JoiningPage(page, view))
}

// joining reads the invitation token opens, and says it is no longer valid
// when it is not: unknown, used or expired alike (D5).
func (s Site) joining(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request, token string, form web.Form) (web.Joining, bool) {
	invitation, err := s.console.Team.Invitation(r.Context(), token)
	if errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid) {
		s.showJoining(w, r, http.StatusGone, web.Joining{Invalid: true})
		return web.Joining{}, false
	}
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "an invitation could not be read", err)
		return web.Joining{}, false
	}
	return web.Joining{Name: invitation.Name, Email: invitation.Email, InvitedBy: invitation.InvitedBy,
		Token: token, Form: form}, true
}

func (s Site) invitationPage(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	token := r.URL.Query().Get("token")
	if view, ok := s.joining(w, r, token, newForm()); ok {
		s.showJoining(w, r, http.StatusOK, view)
	}
}

// acceptInvitation sets the invitee's password, which F13's rules refuse on
// its field, and signs them in, to add an app or a key before anything
// else (D4, D5).
func (s Site) acceptInvitation(w http.ResponseWriter, r *http.Request) {
	token := r.PostFormValue("token")
	session, err := s.console.Team.Accept(r.Context(), visit(r), token, r.PostFormValue("password"))
	if errors.Is(err, staff.ErrInvitationInvalid) {
		s.showJoining(w, r, http.StatusGone, web.Joining{Invalid: true})
		return
	}
	if key, args, shown := formError(err); shown {
		form := newForm()
		form.Error, form.ErrorArgs, form.Field = key, args, "password"
		if view, ok := s.joining(w, r, token, form); ok {
			s.showJoining(w, r, http.StatusUnprocessableEntity, view)
		}
		return
	}
	if err != nil {
		s.failed(w, r, "an invitation could not be accepted", err)
		return
	}
	s.openSession(w, r, session)
	http.Redirect(w, r, "/"+i18n.FromContext(r.Context())+enrolPath, http.StatusSeeOther)
}
```

## Task 19: End-to-end: a colleague invited, joined, signed in with a factor, reset and deactivated, in both languages

The staff pages in a browser, against a fresh database, as the owner and a colleague use them:
the owner makes a role that opens the console and invites a colleague with it for one marketplace; the
link is read from the fake mailbox and opened in a browser context of the colleague's own, where they set
a password and add an app; signed in again with it they see the home, their security and their password,
and not a page more. The owner resets the colleague's factor: the password alone is refused with the
sentence that sends them to the mail, and the reset's link leads to a new app, once. The owner deactivates
them last. The audit log holds each change, and the link's use, with a staff member as its actor.

**Files:**
- Create: `e2e/test_staff.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `e2e/console.py`, the `run_console` fixture, `e2e/accounts.py` (`wait_for_mail`), task 12's
  helpers; tasks 15, 16 and 18's pages.
- Produces: `e2e/test_staff.py`: `test_an_invited_colleague_joins_signs_in_and_is_offboarded[pt-BR|en-US]`,
  `audited(console)`, `add_app(page)`.

- [ ] **Step 1: Write the test**

Create `e2e/test_staff.py`:

```python
"""The console's staff, against a fresh database (F15).

The owner makes a role that opens the console, and invites a colleague with
it for one marketplace; the invitation arrives in the fake mailbox, and its
link, opened in another browser, lets the colleague set a password and add
an app before anything else. Signing in again with the app, the colleague
sees only what the role permits. The link is then no longer valid. The
owner resets the colleague's second factor, which signs them out: their
password alone then opens nothing, and the link the reset mails, with the
password, sends them to add a new app, once. The owner then deactivates
them, after which their password is answered as a wrong one is. Each change
asks for a confirmation, and every one is audited with a staff member as its
actor (docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-26-f15-console-design.md, D5, D9, D10).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

import re

import psycopg
import pytest
from playwright.sync_api import expect, sync_playwright

from accounts import PASSWORD, SIGNED_IN, SUBMIT, sign_in, wait_for_mail
from console import CONSOLE_HOST, first_run
from factors import CODE_SUBMIT, SIGN_OUT, next_totp, totp

# As web/locales words them.
STAFF = {"pt-BR": "Equipe", "en-US": "Staff"}
NO_ROLE = {"pt-BR": "Escolha pelo menos um papel.", "en-US": "Choose at least one role."}
INVITED = {"pt-BR": "O convite foi enviado.", "en-US": "The invitation was sent."}
JOINING = {
    "pt-BR": "Dona convidou você, Colega, para a equipe do console",
    "en-US": "Dona invited you, Colega, to the console's staff",
}
NO_LONGER_VALID = {
    "pt-BR": "Este convite não é mais válido",
    "en-US": "This invitation is no longer valid",
}
IN_STORE = {"pt-BR": "Leitura — em: Loja Um", "en-US": "Leitura — in: Loja Um"}
APP = {"pt-BR": "Aplicativo autenticador", "en-US": "Authenticator app"}
RESET = {"pt-BR": "O segundo fator foi redefinido.", "en-US": "The second factor was reset."}
DEACTIVATED = {"pt-BR": "O membro da equipe foi desativado.", "en-US": "The staff member was deactivated."}
DEACTIVATED_BADGE = {"pt-BR": "Desativado", "en-US": "Deactivated"}
USE_THE_LINK = {
    "pt-BR": "Esta conta não tem segundo fator, e a senha sozinha não adiciona um. Use o link do e-mail que "
             "você recebeu; se ele não for mais válido, peça uma nova redefinição a alguém da equipe que possa "
             "redefinir segundos fatores.",
    "en-US": "This account has no second factor, and the password alone does not add one. Use the link in "
             "the e-mail you received; if it is no longer valid, ask a staff member who can reset second "
             "factors for a new reset.",
}
NEW_FACTOR = {
    "pt-BR": "Colega, o seu segundo fator foi redefinido.",
    "en-US": "Colega, your second factor was reset.",
}
LINK_NO_LONGER_VALID = {
    "pt-BR": "Este link não é mais válido.",
    "en-US": "This link is no longer valid.",
}
REFUSED = {
    "pt-BR": "O e-mail ou a senha não conferem.",
    "en-US": "The e-mail or the password is not right.",
}


def audited(console) -> list[tuple[str, str]]:
    """The staff changes the run audited, and the uses of a reset's link,
    oldest first, with the kind of actor of each: the record's clear columns
    (internal/platform/audit)."""
    with psycopg.connect(console.database.owner_url) as conn:
        return conn.execute(
            "SELECT action, actor_kind FROM audit_log"
            " WHERE action LIKE 'staff.%' OR action = 'identity.factor_reset_link_used' ORDER BY id"
        ).fetchall()


def add_app(page) -> str:
    """Add an authenticator app from the enrolment page the console sends a
    staff member with no second factor to, and return its key."""
    page.click("main a[href$='/account/security/app']")
    key = page.locator("#totp-key").inner_text()
    page.fill("main form input[name=label]", "Celular")
    page.fill("main form input[name=code]", totp(key))
    page.click(CODE_SUBMIT)
    expect(page.locator("ol.codes code")).to_have_count(10)
    return key


@pytest.mark.local_process
@pytest.mark.parametrize("language", ["pt-BR", "en-US"])
def test_an_invited_colleague_joins_signs_in_and_is_offboarded(run_console, screenshots, language):
    console = run_console()
    colleague_address = f"colega-{language.lower()}@example.test"
    home = console.url(CONSOLE_HOST, f"/{language}/")
    with sync_playwright() as p:
        browser = p.chromium.launch()
        owner = browser.new_page()
        first_run(owner, console, language)
        expect(owner).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/enrol$"))
        owner_key = add_app(owner)

        # A role that opens the console and nothing more, made after the
        # second factor is asked again; the step-up then holds ten minutes.
        owner.goto(console.url(CONSOLE_HOST, f"/{language}/roles/new"))
        owner.fill("main form input[name=code]", next_totp(owner_key))
        owner.click(CODE_SUBMIT)
        owner.fill("main form input[name=name]", "Leitura")
        owner.check("input[name=permission][value='console.view']")
        owner.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(owner).to_have_url(re.compile(r"\?done=created$"))

        # The staff, from the menu; and the invitation's form, whose refusal
        # is said on the page.
        owner.goto(home)
        owner.click("nav.menu a[href$='/staff']")
        expect(owner.locator("nav.menu a[aria-current=page]")).to_have_text(STAFF[language])
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-staff-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        owner.click("main a[href$='/staff/invite']")
        owner.fill("main form input[name=name]", "Colega")
        owner.fill("main form input[name=email]", colleague_address)
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-invite-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        owner.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(owner.locator("[role=alert]")).to_have_text(NO_ROLE[language])
        expect(owner.locator("main form input[name=email]")).to_have_value(colleague_address)
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-invite-refused-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        role = owner.locator("input[name=grant]").get_attribute("value")
        owner.check(f"input[name=grant][value='{role}']")
        owner.select_option(f"select[name='scope-{role}']", label="Loja Um")
        owner.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(owner.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(INVITED[language])

        # The link, from the mailbox, in a browser of the colleague's own.
        link = wait_for_mail(console.mailbox, "staff-invitation", colleague_address)["variables"]["Link"]
        assert f"/{language}/invitation?token=" in link, link
        colleague = browser.new_page()
        colleague.goto(link)
        expect(colleague.locator("main p").first).to_contain_text(JOINING[language])
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-invitation-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        colleague.fill("main form input[name=password]", PASSWORD)
        colleague.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/enrol$"))
        colleague_key = add_app(colleague)

        # Signed out and in again, with the app: the console shows the
        # colleague its home, their security and their password, and not a
        # page more.
        colleague.goto(home)
        colleague.click(SIGN_OUT)
        sign_in(colleague, console, colleague_address, host=CONSOLE_HOST, language=language)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(r"/signin/verify$"))
        colleague.fill("main form input[name=code]", next_totp(colleague_key))
        colleague.click(CODE_SUBMIT)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/$"))
        expect(colleague.locator(SIGNED_IN)).to_contain_text("Colega")
        expect(colleague.locator("nav.menu a")).to_have_count(3)
        expect(colleague.locator("nav.menu a[href$='/roles'], nav.menu a[href$='/staff']")).to_have_count(0)
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-invited-console-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        for path in ("/roles", "/staff", "/staff/invite"):
            answer = colleague.goto(console.url(CONSOLE_HOST, f"/{language}{path}"))
            assert answer.status == 404, (path, answer.status)

        # The link worked once.
        answer = colleague.goto(link)
        assert answer.status == 410, answer.status
        expect(colleague.locator("[role=alert]")).to_contain_text(NO_LONGER_VALID[language])
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-invitation-invalid-{language}.png", full_page=True)

        # The owner sees the colleague's role and app, and resets it.
        owner.goto(console.url(CONSOLE_HOST, f"/{language}/staff"))
        member = owner.locator("ul.staff > li", has_text=colleague_address)
        expect(member).to_contain_text(IN_STORE[language])
        expect(member).to_contain_text(APP[language])
        member.locator("a[href$='/reset']").click()
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-staff-reset-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        owner.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(owner.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(RESET[language])

        # Signed out; the password alone opens nothing now, and says where
        # the way back in is: the mailed link, which with the password leads
        # to a new factor, once.
        colleague.goto(home)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(r"/signin$"))
        sign_in(colleague, console, colleague_address, host=CONSOLE_HOST, language=language)
        expect(colleague.locator("[role=alert]")).to_have_text(USE_THE_LINK[language])
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-signin-no-factor-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        reset_link = wait_for_mail(console.mailbox, "second-factor-reset", colleague_address)["variables"]["Link"]
        assert f"/{language}/new-factor?token=" in reset_link, reset_link
        colleague.goto(reset_link)
        expect(colleague.locator("main p").first).to_contain_text(NEW_FACTOR[language])
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-new-factor-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        colleague.fill("main form input[name=password]", PASSWORD)
        colleague.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/enrol$"))
        colleague.goto(home)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(rf"/{language}/enrol$"))
        add_app(colleague)
        answer = colleague.goto(reset_link)
        assert answer.status == 410, answer.status
        expect(colleague.locator("[role=alert]")).to_contain_text(LINK_NO_LONGER_VALID[language])
        colleague.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-new-factor-invalid-{language}.png", full_page=True)

        # Deactivated: signed out, and the password answered as a wrong one.
        owner.locator("ul.staff > li", has_text=colleague_address).locator("a[href$='/deactivate']").click()
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-staff-deactivate-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        owner.click(SUBMIT)
        expect(owner.locator("[role=status]")).to_have_text(DEACTIVATED[language])
        expect(owner.locator("ul.staff > li", has_text=colleague_address).locator(".badge.deactivated")).to_have_text(
            DEACTIVATED_BADGE[language])
        owner.screenshot(path=screenshots / f"f15-staff-deactivated-{language}.png", full_page=True)
        colleague.goto(home)
        expect(colleague).to_have_url(re.compile(r"/signin$"))
        sign_in(colleague, console, colleague_address, host=CONSOLE_HOST, language=language)
        expect(colleague.locator("[role=alert]")).to_have_text(REFUSED[language])
        browser.close()

    assert audited(console) == [
        ("staff.owner_created", "staff"),
        ("staff.role_created", "staff"),
        ("staff.invitation_sent", "staff"),
        ("staff.invitation_accepted", "staff"),
        ("staff.factor_reset", "staff"),
        ("identity.factor_reset_link_used", "staff"),
        ("staff.deactivated", "staff"),
    ]
```

## Task 20: End-to-end: the console's whole story from a fresh database, in both languages, and the audit it leaves

The spec's end-to-end and the roadmap's verification of F15, in one run per language against
a database of its own: the first run, the app and the ten recovery codes; a role that opens the home and
nothing more; an invitation for one marketplace, accepted in a separate browser context; the colleague's
menu and the 404s, `/console/version` included; the reset through the mailed link; the deactivation. The
audit records the run produced are asserted one by one — action, kind of actor and who, subject and which
— in one unbroken chain after the seed's two marketplaces, with no name or address in the clear columns.

```python
def add_app(page) -> str:
    """Add an authenticator app from the enrolment page the console sends a
    staff member with no second factor to, check the ten recovery codes are

def health_version(console) -> str:
    """The build the process says it is, from its health check."""
    with urllib.request.urlopen(console.base_url + "/health", timeout=10) as response:
        return json.load(response)["version"]
```
